## Resources for Your Videos
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: resources-for-your-videos
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
To elevate your content and make it more captivating, consider incorporating a rich tapestry of resources that extend beyond the realm of basic visuals. Beyond simply conveying information, these elements, such as backgrounds, emojis, transitions, and other graphic components, serve as potent tools to infuse depth, personality, and a distinctive flair into your videos or presentations. By strategically integrating these diverse resources, you not only enhance the visual appeal but also enrich the storytelling experience, engage your audience on a deeper level, and strengthen the overall impact of your content.

​

## Backgrounds

Custom backgrounds or overlays can significantly enhance the visual appeal of your content. Whether you’re filming a video or creating a presentation, the right background sets the stage and reinforces your brand’s aesthetic.

Use backgrounds that align with your brand’s color palette and visual identity. This consistency helps in reinforcing brand recognition and creates a cohesive look across your content. You can also create custom backgrounds that feature your logo, slogan, or thematic elements related to your content. This personalization adds a unique touch and strengthens the brand association. Here are some commonly use backgrounds that might help you with your content creation:

**[Video Backgrounds File](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1l0yBT7RS9Vxb_kVa6OsYNC4lXKzgzUf4?usp=sharing)**

​

## Emojis

Emojis are versatile tools that can inject personality into your content and make it more relatable to your audience. They serve as visual elements that can convey emotions, attitudes, and messages more effectively than words alone, such as enthusiasm, humor, or empathy, making your Reels more engaging and relatable.

You can also use emojis to visually highlight key points or important information in your captions, subtitles, or within the video itself. This draws attention to critical messages and encourages viewers to focus on specific details.
​
Finally, select emojis that resonate with your brand’s tone and personality. Whether your brand is playful, professional, or innovative, choose emojis that reinforce these characteristics to maintain consistency. Here are a variety of emoji assets that you can use in your videos:

**[Emojis File](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1hulmACc7A6dNpU1guXRPw2KG8VEOGSXX?usp=sharing)**

## Icons

When it comes to choosing emojis that resonate with your brand’s tone and personality, icons offer a more personalized alternative that can sometimes be more distinctive than emojis, which can feel generic.

Icons can effectively visually represent concepts or actions within your content. They are particularly valuable in representing concepts, helping to clarify information, and increasing engagement.
​

Furthermore, creating your own icons based on your areas of interest can add a much more personalized touch to your posts.

Here are some websites where you can find plenty of icons including 3D and moving elements:

- 
[Lottie Files](https://lottiefiles.com/)

- 
[Jitter](https://jitter.video/)

- 
[Video Effects](https://videoeffects.com/browse/effects)

- 
[Flat Icon](https://www.flaticon.com)

- 
[Lord Icon](https://lordicon.com/icons/)

**[Icons File](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1bh9kSlfXBMYHfpx44_DkbDa1Nd5hxew2?usp=sharing)**

​

## Transitions

Smooth transitions between scenes or slides can improve the flow and continuity of your content. They help maintain viewer interest and guide them through the narrative or information you’re presenting. 
​

Experiment with different types of transitions such as fades, cuts, wipes, or animations. Choose transitions that complement the pace and mood of your content and maintain consistency in your transition styles throughout your video. This creates a polished look and avoids distractions from abrupt changes.
At [Action VFX](https://www.actionvfx.com/collections/water/category) you will be able to find many different transitions for your videos

**[Transitions File](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1HYj5LBzhQw5VF9ZpHf4LJg_2FeGB-LuA?usp=sharing)**

​

## Stock Videos & Movies

Well-selected movie clips can improve the flow and continuity of your content. They help maintain viewer interest and guide them through the narrative or information you’re presenting. Incorporate relevant movie clips or snippets to illustrate key points or evoke specific emotions in your audience. Check the following websites for stock videos & Movie clips

- 
[Bizntech Social AI](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Eye Canndy](https://eyecannndy.com/)

- 
[Get Yarn](https://getyarn.io/)

- 
[Play Phrase](https://www.playphrase.me/)

- 
[Pexels](https://pexels.com)

​

Diversity in graphic resources not only enhances visual appeal but also enriches storytelling and audience engagement. By integrating backgrounds, emojis, transitions, and other elements thoughtfully, you can create content that stands out, resonates with your audience, and effectively communicates your brand’s message. Experiment with different combinations and styles to find what works best for your content and audience.

## Text Hooks
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: text-hooks
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
The hook is a crucial element in your Instagram Reels that grabs viewers' attention within the first few seconds. This initial engagement is essential because the Instagram algorithm favors content that retains viewers, and a strong hook can significantly increase the chances of your video being watched in its entirety, shared, and liked.

With a flood of content competing for attention, your hook is the crucial first point of contact with potential viewers. It acts as your first impression, and as the saying goes, ***"You never get a second chance to make a first impression."*** 

A captivating hook can make the difference between a viewer scrolling past or choosing to invest their time in watching your video. Since Instagram Reels and TikTok videos are typically viewed in an endless scroll format, those initial few seconds are your golden opportunity to capture viewers' interest and draw them in.

Mastering the art of the hook is an essential skill for any content creator aiming to maximize their impact on Instagram Reels. 
​

## Different Types of Text Hooks

- 
**Controversial Hook**: Say the opposite of what you will discuss in the video. For example, "Ugly people get more dates than attractive people," or "A burger has fewer calories than a salad." This unexpected statement grabs attention and piques curiosity.

- 
**Challenge Hook**: Challenge your audience. For instance, "90% of people won't understand what I'm about to say," or "Only 1 in 5 couples stay together for more than 20 years if they do this every day." This type of hook engages viewers by daring them to prove it wrong or understand the concept.

- 
**Bold Statement**: Make a strong, attention-grabbing declaration. For example, "Stop wasting time on these social media mistakes!" This hook immediately tells the viewer that valuable, time-saving information will follow.

- 
**Surprising Element**: Show something unusual or surprising. For instance, "You won't believe what happened when I tried this new technique!" This type of hook intrigues viewers, making them want to watch more to satisfy their curiosity.

- 
**Personal Story**: Start with a personal anecdote that leads into the main content. For example, "I lost 20 pounds by following these simple steps, and you can too!" This hook makes the content relatable and engaging.

**Question Hook**: Ask a provocative question to engage the audience. For instance, "Do you know why most people fail at their New Year's resolutions?" This encourages viewers to keep watching for the answer.

- 
**Shock Value**: Begin with a shocking fact or statistic. For instance, "Did you know that 70% of people are making this mistake daily?" This hook grabs attention through surprise and urgency.
​

*People desire content that either benefits them, challenges their beliefs, entertains them, or makes them think differently*

​

## Font Styles
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: font-styles
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
Here is a comprehensive guide of fonts that may be useful for you. Many of these fonts come in various versions, including light, italic, and bold, allowing you to experiment and find the perfect fit for your needs. By playing with these variants, you can add depth and variety to your text.

Additionally, an interesting approach is to use two different fonts for your titles and subtitles. This technique can help differentiate between sections and create a visually appealing hierarchy in your content. When you want to highlight very important words, using contrasting fonts can draw attention and emphasize key points effectively.

A useful trick for identifying fonts is to take a screenshot of text from a content creator whose style you admire. Once you have the screenshot, you can upload it to [WhatTheFont](https://www.myfonts.com/es/pages/whatthefont). This tool will provide you with a variety of similar fonts, allowing you to choose the closest match to the original. It’s a great way to discover new fonts and maintain a consistent aesthetic in your projects.

Once you've identified a similar font, you can then visit websites like [DaFont](https://www.dafont.com/), [Google Fonts](https://fonts.google.com/), [BeFonts](https://befonts.com/), or [Font Download](https://font.download/). At these sites, you can search for the font by name and download it for free. 

Moreover, this technique is incredibly straightforward and can save you a lot of time searching for the perfect font.

## Some additional tips on using fonts effectively

- 
**Consistency is Key:** Stick to a consistent set of fonts throughout your Reels to maintain a cohesive look. This helps in establishing your brand identity and makes your content recognizable.

- 
**Readable Sizes:** Ensure that your text is easily readable on small screens. Avoid using fonts that are too small or overly decorative, as they might be hard to read on mobile devices.

- 
**High Contrast:** Use high contrast between your text and background to ensure visibility. Light fonts on dark backgrounds or dark fonts on light backgrounds work best.

- 
**Highlight Key Points:** Use bold or italic variants to highlight important information or calls to action. This helps in drawing the viewer's attention to crucial parts of your message.

- 
**Engage with Styles:** Mix and match different font styles to keep your content engaging. For instance, use a playful font for captions and a clean, sans-serif font for informational text.

- 
**Animate Text:** Use text animations to make your Reels more dynamic. Animated text can capture attention and make your content more engaging.

## Font Usage Tips

- 
**Vintage Fonts:** Use vintage fonts when you want to evoke nostalgia or a retro feel. They are perfect for themed events, vintage products, or to give a timeless look to your content.

- 
**Minimalist Fonts:** Minimalist fonts are clean and simple, making them perfect for modern and elegant designs. Use them for content that requires a sophisticated and uncluttered look.

- 
**Playful Fonts:** Use playful, whimsical fonts for informal content, children's materials, or any context where you want to convey fun and creativity.

- 
**Bold Fonts:** Bold fonts are ideal for headlines, titles, or any text that you want to stand out. They are great for making a strong statement or emphasizing important points.

## Ready to share August/ September content ideas!
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: ready-to-share-august-september-content-ideas
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
## Hey there, Bizntech Social Community,

As summer winds down, we’re approaching some significant dates that offer both a moment of reflection and a chance to engage your audience with meaningful content. August and September are filled with opportunities to create posts that resonate on a deeper level with your followers. Whether it's honoring historical contributions or having some fun with superstitions, these upcoming days have something for everyone.

At Bizntech Social, we know how vital it is to stay relevant and connected. That’s why we’ve put together a list of content ideas tailored to these special dates, ensuring you have everything you need to make an impact.

## August 18: National Navajo Code Talkers Day

National Navajo Code Talkers Day is a day to honor the Navajo Code Talkers, a group of Native Americans who played a pivotal role in World War II by using their native language to create an unbreakable code. This day is an opportunity to recognize their bravery and the unique cultural contributions they made.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**#1. Share a historical fact or story.** Example: A history-focused account could post a series of images or infographics detailing the role of the Navajo Code Talkers in key battles.

- 
**#2. Highlight the importance of language and culture.** Example: A language school could share a video on the significance of preserving indigenous languages, linking it to the story of the Navajo Code Talkers.

- 
**#3. Post a tribute or thank you. ** Example: A government related account could post a simple “Thank You” graphic, acknowledging the service and contributions of the Navajo Code Talkers.

[*](assets/images/media_34.jpeg)

## August 26: Women’s Equality Day

Women’s Equality Day commemorates the anniversary of the 19th Amendment, which granted women the right to vote in the United States. It’s a day to celebrate the achievements of women and advocate for gender equality.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**#1. Celebrate women leaders in your industry.** Example: Highlight the achievements of women in your company or industry with a series of short profiles or interviews.

- 
**#2. Share a timeline of women’s rights milestones.** Example: Create a post that educates your audience on key events in the women’s rights movement, from the 19th Amendment to today.

- 
**#3. Host a discussion on gender equality.** Example: Organize a live chat or panel discussion on Instagram or LinkedIn, featuring influential women from your industry discussing ongoing challenges and progress in gender equality.

[](assets/images/media_35.jpeg)

## September 2: Labor Day

Labor Day is a federal holiday in the United States dedicated to the contributions of workers to the American economy. It’s a perfect time to reflect on the value of hard work and to give your team some well-deserved recognition.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**#1. Spotlight your team’s hard work.** Example: Share behind-the-scenes photos of your team in action, or create a video highlighting their achievements over the past year.

- 
**#2. Discuss the importance of work-life balance.** Example: Post tips on how to maintain a healthy work-life balance, encouraging your audience to take time for themselves as well.

- 
**#3. Engage with your audience about their work experiences.** Example: Host a poll asking your audience what they love most about their jobs or share stories of how their work impacts their lives and communities.

[](assets/images/media_36.jpeg)

## **September 5: International Day of Charity**

This day highlights the importance of charitable efforts in reducing poverty and suffering worldwide. It’s an opportunity to showcase how giving back can make a difference in communities everywhere.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**Feature a charity:** Highlight a cause your business supports or encourage your audience to donate. Example*: A company could partner with a charity and run a campaign, with a portion of profits going to the cause.

- 
**Share stories of impact:** Post success stories from charities your audience can relate to. *Example*: A retail brand could share customer stories about how their purchases have helped support charitable causes.

- 
**Organize a charity event:** Use this day to kick off a fundraiser or volunteer initiative. *Example*: A community center could organize a local event and encourage participation through social media.

[*](assets/images/media_37.jpeg)

## September 13: Friday the 13th

Friday the 13th is a day shrouded in superstition and mystery. It’s a great opportunity to engage with your audience in a light-hearted and fun way, especially if your brand has a playful side.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**#1. Share fun superstitions related to your industry.** Example: A tech company could post about common superstitions in tech, like the infamous “demo gods” who seem to cause software glitches during presentations.

- 
**#2. Run a “lucky vs. unlucky” poll.** Example: Engage your audience with a poll on whether they believe in Friday the 13th superstitions, sharing fun facts about the day’s history.

- 
**#3. Create a “spooky” themed post.** Example: A retail brand could offer a Friday the 13th discount code, playing into the day’s spooky reputation.

[](assets/images/media_38.jpeg)

## **September 27: World Tourism Day**

World Tourism Day, organized by the United Nations, is dedicated to highlighting the importance of tourism and its social, cultural, political, and economic value. It’s a day to celebrate how travel opens doors, broadens perspectives, and contributes to global development.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**Promote local tourism:** Share hidden gems or must-visit spots in your local area. Example*: A local business could post a guide to lesser-known attractions in their town or city, encouraging locals to explore their own backyard.

- 
**Highlight sustainable travel:** Educate your audience on eco-friendly travel practices and how they can minimize their impact while exploring the world. *Example*: A travel agency could share tips on how to travel sustainably, from choosing eco-friendly accommodations to supporting local economies.

- 
**Feature travel stories:** Ask your audience to share their favorite travel memories or destinations. *Example*: A hotel chain could run a social media campaign where customers post photos from their stays, using a specific hashtag for a chance to win a future trip.

✏️ **Real Example:** A travel blog could create a series of posts featuring interviews with travelers who have explored unique destinations, highlighting the cultural experiences that had the most impact on them.

[*](assets/images/media_39.jpeg)
## **September 29: International Day of Awareness of Food Loss and Waste**

This day, recognized by the United Nations, focuses on raising awareness about the importance of reducing food loss and waste. It highlights the impact of food waste on food security, climate change, and environmental sustainability.

💡 **Content Ideas:**

- 
**Share tips on reducing food waste:** Provide practical advice on how individuals and businesses can minimize food waste in their daily lives. Example*: A grocery store could share a guide on how to properly store fruits and vegetables to extend their shelf life, along with recipes for using leftovers creatively.

- 
**Promote local initiatives:** Highlight local programs or organizations working to reduce food waste in your community. *Example*: A restaurant could collaborate with a local food bank to donate surplus food and share this initiative with their audience to inspire others to contribute.

- 
**Educational posts:** Explain the environmental and economic impacts of food waste, encouraging your audience to be mindful of their food consumption habits. *Example*: A sustainability-focused brand could post infographics or short videos showing the journey of food waste from the kitchen to the landfill, and how small changes can make a big difference.

✏️ **Real Example:** A zero-waste organization could run a campaign featuring real stories of families or businesses that have successfully implemented strategies to reduce their food waste, showcasing the positive outcomes.

[](assets/images/media_40.jpeg)

We hope these ideas inspire you to create impactful content that resonates with your audience. If you have other cool ideas or need more inspiration, just hit reply and let us know.

Cheers, Bizntech Social Team

## Christmas Story Templates
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: christmas-story-templates
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

Stories are perfect for strengthening the connection between your existing audience and your brand. They allow you to showcase the human side of your business, build trust, and turn followers into potential customers.

**Stories are perfect for nurturing and selling to your followers.** They are an excellent way to move your followers from the [first step of the funnel](#/article/social-media-strategy-sales-funnel) (visitors) to the second (leads). However, it’s not enough for people to just watch your stories; it’s crucial to encourage interaction. This way you can:

- 
**Identify potential customers**: When someone responds to a poll, votes on a sticker, or interacts with your story, you can identify them and reach out directly.

- 
**Improve your reach**: When users interact, the algorithm recognizes them as "interested users," prioritizing your future posts and content in their feeds.

- 
**Increase your chances of going viral**: The more engagement your stories receive, the greater the opportunity to reach a wider audience through the algorithm.

The holiday season is the perfect time to stand out with creative content aligned with the festive spirit, encouraging your audience to engage with your brand.

## Importance of Festive Content

Content tailored to special occasions like Christmas has a stronger impact for several reasons:

- 
**Emotional connection**: During the holidays, people are more receptive to warm, relatable messages. Creating content that aligns with this spirit helps you empathize with your audience.

- 
**Cultural relevance**: Showing that your brand is in tune with important moments of the year reinforces your authenticity and connection.

- 
**Higher engagement**: Festive topics tend to attract more attention on social media, as people look for content that reflects their mood and experiences.

- 
**Competitive differentiation**: In a crowded market, tailoring your messages to the season helps you stand out from the rest.

- 
**Sales booster**: For businesses, contextual content encourages purchases by linking your products or services to the specific needs of the season.

To help your content shine this holiday season, I’m sharing **ready-to-use Christmas story templates** that you can easily download and upload without any editing. They’re designed to help your audience interact with your brand in a unique and original way

[Download Story Templates](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1OgAAb-8IEgNmi8n0l33J3v34KouTtj_k?usp=drive_link)
*Don't forget to tag us in your stories so we can give you a 'like' and interact with your content *😉

# Versión en Español

Las historias son perfectas para fortalecer la conexión entre tu audiencia actual y tu marca. Estas te permiten mostrar el lado humano de tu negocio, generar confianza y convertir a tus seguidores en clientes potenciales.

**Las historias son ideales para nutrir y vender a tus seguidores.** Son una excelente herramienta para mover a tu audiencia del [primer paso del embudo](#/article/social-media-strategy-sales-funnel) (visitantes) al segundo (leads). Sin embargo, no basta con que las personas solo vean tus historias; es crucial fomentar la interacción.

- 
**Identifica clientes potenciales**: Cuando alguien responde a una encuesta, vota en un sticker o interactúa con tu historia, puedes identificarlos y contactarlos directamente.

- 
**Mejora tu alcance**: Cuando los usuarios interactúan, el algoritmo los reconoce como "usuarios interesados", priorizando tus futuros contenidos en su feed.

- 
**Aumenta tus posibilidades de viralización**: Cuanta más interacción reciban tus historias, mayores serán las oportunidades de alcanzar una audiencia más amplia gracias al algoritmo.

La temporada navideña es el momento perfecto para destacar con contenido creativo alineado con el espíritu festivo y fomentar la interacción de tu audiencia con tu marca.

## **Importancia del Contenido Festivo**

El contenido adaptado a ocasiones especiales como la Navidad tiene un impacto más fuerte por varias razones:

- 
**Conexión emocional**: Durante las fiestas, las personas son más receptivas a mensajes cálidos y relacionados. Crear contenido alineado con este espíritu te ayuda a empatizar con tu audiencia.

- 
**Relevancia cultural**: Mostrar que tu marca está en sintonía con momentos importantes del año refuerza tu autenticidad y conexión.

- 
**Mayor engagement**: Los temas festivos tienden a captar más atención en redes sociales, ya que las personas buscan contenido que refleje su estado de ánimo y experiencias.

- 
**Diferenciación competitiva**: En un mercado saturado, adaptar tus mensajes a la temporada te ayuda a destacar.

- 
**Impulsor de ventas**: Para los negocios, el contenido contextual fomenta las compras al vincular tus productos o servicios con las necesidades específicas de la temporada.

Para que tu contenido brille esta temporada, te comparto **templates navideños listos para usar en tus historias**. Puedes descargarlos fácilmente y subirlos sin necesidad de edición. Están diseñados para que tu audiencia interactúe con tu marca de una manera única y original.

## Video Formats for Your Reels
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: video-formats-for-your-reels
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
Social Media videos are one of the most powerful ways to grow your brand, boost engagement, and connect with your audience. But not all videos perform the same. Some formats grab attention instantly, while others get lost in the scroll. The key? Use a mix of different styles to keep your content fresh, engaging, and aligned with your brand.
​

Below, we break down **the top video formats** you should be using, along with examples to help you get started. Whether you prefer being on camera or creating faceless videos, there's a format for you!

## Different ways to record a video 

### **1. Talking Head Reels**

- 
You, facing the camera, **dropping knowledge** like a pro.

- 
Simple, engaging, and **perfect for personal branding**.

- 
Example: "3 mistakes killing your Instagram engagement!"

### **2. Text on Screen Reels**

- 
No talking, just **text popping up on beat** with a trending sound.

- 
Works great for **quick tips or relatable content**.

- 
Example: "POV: You’re a social media manager and your client ignores your strategy."

### **3. Trend-Jacking Reels**

- 
Hop on **viral trends, sounds, and challenges** with text overlays

- 
Great for **engagement and relatability**.

- 
Example: Using the “I’m just a baby” sound to joke about new clients.

### **4. Green Screen Reels**

- 
You in front of a **screenshot, website, or tweet** explaining something.

- 
Super engaging & informative.

- 
Example: "Breaking down Instagram’s latest update!"

### **5. Lip Sync or Voiceover Reels**

- 
Use **funny or trending audio** and **apply it to your niche**.

- 
**Super viral potential** if done right.

- 
Example: Using the “It’s Corn” song but making it about social media marketing.

### 6. Faceless

- 
Use stock video, images, transitions, sound effects, and animations to create the video.

- 
Use your own background voice or the voice created by an AI.

🔥 **BONUS:** **Hybrid Reels** – Combine **2 or more** of these formats (e.g., Green Screen + Talking Head + B-Roll) for extra engagement.

## **How to Make These Go Viral**

✅ Keep your content fresh by varying your video formats. Repeating the same ones can bore your audience.

✅ Use these ideas in combination with different [video topics for your Reels](#/article/video-topics-for-your-reels).

✅ Use a [good script structure](#/article/structure-of-a-viral-video) to keep your audience hooked

✅ If you record yourself (and don’t make faceless videos), [use different camera angles.](#/article/most-common-video-shots-for-reels)

## **🎬 Time to Take Action!**

Now that you know the best video formats, it’s time to put them into action! Test different styles, see what resonates with your audience, and don’t be afraid to experiment. The more variety you bring to your content, the better your chances of standing out and going viral.

Which format will you try first? Let us know! 🚀

📌 Need help scheduling, analyzing, or automating your social media? **[Bizntech Social](https://bizntechstl.com) makes content creation effortless.** Try it today and take your Reels strategy to the next level!

## Video Topics for Your Reels
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: video-topics-for-your-reels
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
If you're a **social media manager**, you already know that videos are one of the **fastest ways to grow**, but what happens when you **run out of ideas**? Or worse, when your content **doesn’t get engagement**? 😬

The good news? **Videos don’t have to be complicated.** With the right strategy, you can create engaging, shareable content that attracts clients, grows your brand, and keeps your audience hooked.

That’s why we’ve put together **30 high-performing video ideas** specifically for social media managers. Whether you’re into **educational content, relatable memes, storytelling, or trend-jacking**, this list has something for you. Plus, each idea comes with a real-world example, so you’ll **never feel stuck again**. 💪

## 
30 Topics for Videos

#

🎥 **Video Type**

🎯 **How It Works**

🔥 **Why It Works**

Example

1

**Behind-the-Scenes (BTS)**

Show how you create content, edit, or manage social media accounts

People LOVE seeing the process

**"A Day in the Life of a Social Media Manager"** – Quick time-lapse of scheduling posts, checking analytics, and responding to comments.

2

**Tutorial & Educational**

Step-by-step how-tos, tips, and hacks

Huge shareability factor

"**How to Write Viral Hooks in Under 10 Seconds"** – Show examples of viral hooks with text overlays.

3

**Before & After**

Show a transformation. Whether it’s growth stats, branding glow-ups, or content upgrades

People LOVE seeing progress and results

"**Before vs. After Fixing My Client’s Instagram Bio"** – Side-by-side of an optimized vs. unoptimized bio.

4

**Meme & Relatable**

Use humor, trending audios, or relatable situations to connect with your audience.

People share these like crazy

"**Me Explaining the Algorithm to a Client for the 100th Time"** – Lip sync to a funny soundbite."

5

**Storytelling & POV**

**Te**ll a short, engaging story that hooks people in

Personal experiences & struggles work best

"**That One Time a Client Went Viral by Accident"** – Use a green screen to show the post that blew up.

6

**Industry Trend**

Share breaking news, updates, or predictions in your industry.

People love staying ahead of trends.

"**Instagram Just Changed the Algorithm… Again!"** – React to new Instagram updates with a green screen background.

7

**Common Mistakes**

Highlight mistakes people make in your niche & how to fix them.

High engagement, relatable, & valuable.

"**3 Mistakes Killing Your Engagement (And How to Fix Them)"** – Show common errors with examples.

8

**Myth-Busting**

Debunk common myths or misconceptions in your field.

Grabs attention & sparks discussion.

"**Posting More = More Engagement? Not Always!"** – Explain why quality > quantity in content creation.

9

**What I Wish I Knew**

Share lessons you learned the hard way.

Personal, relatable & shareable.

"**What I Wish I Knew Before Becoming a Social Media Manager"** – Drop 3-5 knowledge bombs.

10

**Quick Hacks**

Share a game-changing shortcut or tip in seconds.

Super digestible, high saveability.

"**Hack to Find the Best Hashtags in Under 30 Seconds"** – Screen record an easy hashtag research method.

11

**Hot Takes & Controversial Opinions**

Share a bold, unpopular opinion (with facts).

Creates buzz & conversation.

"**You Don’t Need to Post Every Day to Grow"** – Explain why consistency > frequency.

12

**Tool & Resource**

Recommend apps, websites, or tools that make life easier.

People love discovering helpful resources.

"**3 Free Tools Every Social Media Manager Needs"** – Show tools like Canva, CapCut, and Metricool.

13

**Client Win or Testimonial**

Showcase success stories or results from your work.

Social proof = credibility boost.

"**How I Helped a Client Grow from 1K to 10K Followers in 3 Months"** – Show before & after analytics.

14

**Challenge & Trend-Jacking**

Hop on viral challenges or trends, but add a niche twist.

High chance of virality.

"**POV: When the Instagram Algorithm Is Finally on Your Side"** – Use a trending audio to joke about a post going viral.

15

**FAQ & Q&A**

Answer common questions from your audience.

Builds authority & connection.

"**How Do I Get More Clients as a Social Media Manager?"** – Share your best networking & outreach tips.

16

**Day in the Life**

Show a realistic day in your job/life.

People love behind-the-scenes content.

"**A Day in My Life as a Freelance Social Media Manager"** – Mix B-roll clips of work tasks.

17

**Client Red Flags**

Call out warning signs of bad clients (in a fun way).

Relatable, shareable & starts conversations.

"**Red Flags That a Client Will Be a Nightmare"** – Share 3-5 warning signs.

18

**If I Started Over**

Share what you'd do differently if you had to start from scratch.

People LOVE expert insights & shortcuts.

"**If I Had to Start as a Social Media Manager from Scratch, I’d Do This"** – Give your best tips for beginners.

19

**Productivity & Time Management**

Share hacks to work smarter, not harder.

High saveability factor.

"**How I Manage 5+ Social Media Accounts Without Burning Out"** – Show your workflow.

20

**What NOT to Do**

Highlight common pitfalls in your industry.

Reverse psychology = great engagement.

"**STOP Doing This in Your Instagram Captions!"** – Explain common caption mistakes.

21

**Pricing & Money Talk **

Discuss rates, pricing strategies, or financial mistakes.

Money content **always** does well. 💸

"**How to Price Your Social Media Management Services (No Undervaluing!)"** – Share a simple pricing formula.

22

**Reaction & Duet**

React to industry trends, viral clips, or tweets.

Fun, easy, and taps into existing viral content.

"**Reacting to an Influencer's Cringe Social Media Advice"** – Green screen reaction to a bad social media tip.

23

**Exposing Industry Secrets**

Reveal hidden truths people don’t talk about.

People love insider knowledge.

"**The Truth About Instagram Growth No One Tells You"** – Drop real talk on growth hacks.

24

**Step-by-Step Case Study**

Walk through a project transformation.

Before & afters with a strategy? Gold.

"**How I Turned a Failing Account Into a 6-Figure Brand"** – Breakdown with screenshots.

25

**Worst Advice You’ve Heard**

Call out bad industry advice & explain why.

Controversial + educational = views.

"**Worst Advice I Got as a Social Media Manager"** – Reaction format, facepalm vibes.

26

**Personal Wins & Lessons**

Share a big win & the key takeaways from it.

Authenticity + value = trust.

"**How I Landed My Biggest Client Yet – Here’s How!"** – Storytelling + actionable tips.

27

**Would You Rather?**

Present two tough choices related to your industry.

Easy engagement booster.

"**Would You Rather Go Viral Once or Have Steady Growth?"** – Poll-style engagement.

28

**Storytelling with Screenshots**

Show a viral tweet, DM, or email & react to it.

Super easy, and people **eat up** drama.

**"This Client Thought 1 Post a Week Was Enough for Growth 🤦‍♀️"** – Screenshot + reaction.

29

**Mini Vlog-Style**

Show short clips from your workday with aesthetic edits.

Engaging, easy, and keeps people watching.

"**A Chill Workday as a Social Media Manager ☕💻"** – Soft music, cozy vibes.

30

**Things I Wish More People Knew**

Drop underrated tips or truths about your field.

High saveability & shareability.

"**No One Talks About This Social Media Growth Hack…"** – Hook + knowledge drop.

## **How to Make These Go Viral**

✅ Use a [good script structure](#/article/structure-of-a-viral-video) to keep your audience hooked

✅ Keep your content fresh by varying your video topics. Repeating the same ones can bore your audience.

✅ Use these ideas in combination with different [video formats for your Reels.](#/article/video-formats-for-your-reels)

✅ If you record yourself (and don’t make faceless videos), [use different camera angles.](#/article/most-common-video-shots-for-reels)

## Most Common Video Shots for Reels
**Category**: Social Media Marketing Course
**Reference ID**: most-common-video-shots-for-reels
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
If you’ve ever watched a video and felt hooked from the first few seconds, chances are, the camera angles played a huge part in that feeling. The way a scene is shot can completely change how we experience it. Whether it’s making us feel more connected to a character, highlighting an important moment, or adding a sense of drama. 

As a creator, knowing when and how to use different types of shots can make your content stand out. In this guide, we’ll dive into the most common shots and how each one can help take your videos to the next level. Let’s explore how small changes in perspective can lead to a big impact on your audience.

Here are 3 influencers who use these techniques in their content:

- 
[Alcanza tu meta](https://vm.tiktok.com/ZMkteAEXt/)

- 
[Themonkeyc](https://vm.tiktok.com/ZMkteX4K7/)

- 
[Nomadsrec](https://vm.tiktok.com/ZMkteVedV/)

## **Video Shots**

All of these shots can be used with either the camera or the character in motion, as well as in static positions.

**#**

**Shot name**

**What it is used for**

1

**Nadir Shot**

This shot is used to emphasize the character

2

**Overhead Shot**

✔️ It provides an overall view of the scene.

✔️ It shows the development of an action.

✔️ It breaks the pattern of other shots since it’s not used frequently.

3

**High Angle Shot**

This creates a sense of superiority for the viewer.

4

**Eye Level Shot**

Filming at eye level makes the viewer feel as if they are on the same level as the subject.

5

**Dutch Angle**

Often used in chaotic situations

6

**Wide Shot**

✔️ It captures the entire environment.

✔️ It gives context to the story or what you’re conveying.

7

**Close-Up Shot**

✔️ It highlights the character’s emotions.

✔️ It shows the details of an object.

✔️ It captures the development of a specific action.

8

**Point of View (POV)**

The viewer feels as though they are the character

9

**Medium Shot**

It’s used to show the environment and provide context, similar to the wide shot but with more intimacy.

10

**Low Angle Medium Shot**

This shot is used to highlight the development of an action

By incorporating a variety of shots, you can transform your videos into dynamic, visually compelling stories that captivate your audience. Remember, the key to keeping your viewers engaged is to be intentional with every frame. So, experiment with different angles and perspectives, and don’t be afraid to step outside the typical patterns. With these techniques at your disposal, you’ll be well on your way to creating content that not only captures attention but also leaves a lasting impact.

## **How to Make These Go Viral**

✅ Keep your content fresh by varying your video shots. Repeating the same ones can bore your audience.

✅ Use these ideas in combination with different [video formats for your Reels.](#/article/video-formats-for-your-reels)

✅ Use viral ideas as your [video topics for your Reels](#/article/video-topics-for-your-reels)

✅ Use a [good script structure](#/article/structure-of-a-viral-video) to keep your audience hooked

Tu cuenta de Pinterest ahora está integrada con Bizntech Social. Puedes comenzar a gestionar tus tableros de Pinterest, programar pines y rastrear el rendimiento directamente desde la plataforma Bizntech Social.

## **TikTok**

- 
Inicia sesión en tu cuenta de Bizntech Social.

- 
Navega a la sección "Integraciones" en la configuración de tu cuenta.

- 
Haz clic en el logo de TikTok en la página principal o en el botón "Integrar TikTok" en configuraciones.
[](assets/images/media_66.png)
- 
Serás redirigido a la página de autorización de TikTok. Ingresa tus credenciales de cuenta de TikTok y autoriza a Bizntech Social a acceder a tu cuenta.
[](assets/images/media_67.png)
- 
Una vez autorizado, serás redirigido de nuevo a Bizntech Social.

- 
¡Felicidades! Tu cuenta de TikTok ahora está integrada con Bizntech Social.

## Managing multiple social media accounts on Bizntech Social
**Category**: Integrations
**Reference ID**: managing-multiple-social-media-accounts-on-bizntech-social
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

Managing multiple social media accounts can be a daunting task, but with Bizntech Social, it becomes a breeze. Bizntech Social offers the Pro plan and the Agency plan, which allow multiple users to create and schedule content across various social media accounts. Additionally, Bizntech Social allows users to manage multiple companies, which can represent products or services, each with their own social media accounts.

To get started with Bizntech Social, each company must go through an onboarding process that provides Bizntech Social with the necessary information to understand the company's social media strategy and goals. Once onboarded, each company can connect/integrate with all the social media accounts that Bizntech Social supports, allowing for streamlined content creation and scheduling.

For users on the Pro or Agency plan, they can view the metrics and reports for all social media accounts managed by their users. This feature allows for easy management of multiple companies and social media accounts, making it an ideal solution for agencies or businesses with multiple brands.

To manage multiple social media accounts on Bizntech Social, follow these steps:

- 
Sign up for the Pro or Agency plan on Bizntech Social

- 
Complete the onboarding process for each company you manage

- 
Connect/integrate each company's social media accounts with Bizntech Social

- 
Have users create and schedule content for each company's social media accounts

- 
View metrics and reports for all social media accounts managed by your users

By following these steps, managing multiple social media accounts becomes a breeze with Bizntech Social.

# Versión en Español

Gestionar múltiples cuentas de redes sociales puede ser una tarea abrumadora, pero con Bizntech Social, se convierte en algo sencillo. Bizntech Social ofrece el plan Pro y el plan Agency, que permiten a varios usuarios crear y programar contenido en diversas cuentas de redes sociales. Además, Bizntech Social permite a los usuarios gestionar varias empresas, las cuales pueden representar productos o servicios, cada una con sus propias cuentas de redes sociales.

Para comenzar con Bizntech Social, cada empresa debe pasar por un proceso de incorporación que proporcione a Bizntech Social la información necesaria para entender la estrategia y los objetivos de redes sociales de la empresa. Una vez completada la incorporación, cada empresa puede conectar/integrar todas las cuentas de redes sociales que Bizntech Social admite, lo que permite una creación y programación de contenido más efficient.

Para los usuarios con el plan Pro o Agency, pueden ver las métricas e informes de todas las cuentas de redes sociales gestionadas por sus usuarios. Esta función facilita la gestión de múltiples empresas y cuentas de redes sociales, lo que lo convierte en una solución ideal para agencias o negocios con múltiples marcas.

Para gestionar varias cuentas de redes sociales en Bizntech Social, sigue estos pasos:

- 
Regístrate en el plan Pro o Agency en Bizntech Social

- 
Completa el proceso de incorporación para cada empresa que gestiones

- 
Conecta/integra las cuentas de redes sociales de cada empresa con Bizntech Social

- 
Permite que los usuarios creen y programen contenido para las cuentas de redes sociales de cada empresa

- 
Visualiza métricas e informes de todas las cuentas de redes sociales gestionadas por tus usuarios

Siguiendo estos pasos, gestionar múltiples cuentas de redes sociales se vuelve sencillo con Bizntech Social.

## How to connect to a different Social Media Account
**Category**: Integrations
**Reference ID**: how-to-connect-to-a-different-social-media-account
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

This is most likely because you have that account open in your browser, which saves cookies to keep the session open. You can easily check this by opening a new tab in your browser right now and typing in the URL of the social network you're trying to connect to, for example: facebook.com. You'll see that you're already logged in. 

Bizntech Social works in the same way, which is why when you remove the integration of a social network and reconnect it, it connects back to the same account.

But don't worry, it's very easy to sort this out. Below are two options:

## Option A

- 
Open a new tab in the same browser you are using for Bizntech Social.

- 
Enter the URL of the social network where you want to change the account, for example, tiktok.com (if the social network is Instagram, you should go to facebook.com since IG is managed through your personal FB account).

- 
You'll see that you're logged in. Log out of the session.

- 
Go to Bizntech Social's [integrations section](https://bizntechstl.com) and disconnect the social network.

- 
In the same section of Bizntech Social, re-integrate the social network.

- 
This time it should ask you to log in with your username and password. Log in with the correct account this time.

## Option B

- 
Open an **incognito** window in your browser. 

- 
Sign in to Bizntech Social. 

- 
Go to the [Integrations section](https://bizntechstl.com) and disconnect the social network you are trying to change. 

- 
In the same section of Bizntech Social, re-integrate the social network. 

- 
This time it should ask you to log in with your username and password. Log in with the correct account.

If your **personal Facebook** account logged into Bizntech Social is correct but you're not seeing your Instagram pages or accounts listed as options, then I recommend reading these two articles:

​[Confirm the correct level of access to your Facebook page](#/article/troubleshooting-confirming-correct-level-of-access-for-your-facebook-page)

[How to add a Facebook page to your professional Instagram account](#/article/how-to-add-a-facebook-page-to-your-professional-instagram-account)

It's important to understand that both the Facebook page and the professional Instagram account are linked to the same **personal Facebook **account. That's why when you first log in to Instagram or Facebook, it asks you to connect your **personal Facebook **account.

# Versión en Español

¿Te has conectado con una cuenta de una red social y cada vez que intentas cambiarla se vuelve a conectar con la misma? Aquí tienes la solución...

Esto se debe probablemente a que tienes esa cuenta abierta en tu navegador, lo que guarda cookies para mantener la sesión abierta. Puedes verificar esto fácilmente abriendo una nueva pestaña en tu navegador ahora mismo y escribiendo la URL de la red social a la que estás tratando de conectarte, por ejemplo: facebook.com. Verás que ya estás conectado.
Bizntech Social funciona de la misma manera, por lo que cuando eliminas la integración de una red social y la vuelves a conectar, se conecta a la misma cuenta.
​

Pero no te preocupes, es muy fácil solucionar esto. A continuación, se presentan dos opciones:

​

## **Opción A**

- 
Abre una nueva pestaña en el mismo navegador que estás utilizando para Bizntech Social.

- 
Ingresa la URL de la red social donde deseas cambiar la cuenta, por ejemplo, tiktok.com (si la red social es Instagram, deberías ir a facebook.com, ya que IG se gestiona a través de tu cuenta personal de FB).

- 
Verás que estás conectado. Cierra la sesión.

- 
Ve a la sección de integraciones de Bizntech Social y desconecta la red social.

- 
En la misma sección de Bizntech Social, reintegra la red social.

Esta vez debería pedirte que inicies sesión con tu nombre de usuario y contraseña. Inicia sesión con la cuenta correcta esta vez.

## **Opción B**

- 
Abre una ventana de incógnito en tu navegador.

- 
Inicia sesión en Bizntech Social.

- 
Ve a la sección de Integraciones y desconecta la red social que estás intentando cambiar.

- 
En la misma sección de Bizntech Social, reintegra la red social.

Esta vez debería pedirte que inicies sesión con tu nombre de usuario y contraseña. Inicia sesión con la cuenta correct.

Si tu cuenta personal de Facebook que inició sesión en Bizntech Social es correcta pero no ves tus páginas o cuentas de Instagram listadas como opciones, te recomiendo leer estos dos artículos:

- 
[Confirma el nivel de acceso correcto a tu página de Facebook](#/article/troubleshooting-confirming-correct-level-of-access-for-your-facebook-page)

- 
[Cómo agregar una página de Facebook a tu cuenta profesional de Instagram](#/article/how-to-add-a-facebook-page-to-your-professional-instagram-account)

Es importante entender que tanto la página de Facebook como la cuenta profesional de Instagram están vinculadas a la misma cuenta personal de Facebook. Por eso, cuando inicias sesión por primera vez en Instagram o Facebook, te pide que conectes tu cuenta personal de Facebook.

## Resolving Instagram and Facebook Integration issues
**Category**: Integrations
**Reference ID**: resolving-instagram-and-facebook-integration-issues
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

It's important to understand that both the Facebook page and the professional Instagram account are linked to the same **personal Facebook **account. That's why when you first log in to Instagram or Facebook, it asks you to connect your **personal Facebook **account.

## 

Facebook Issues

If you are not the owner of the Facebook page, make sure to have the correct level of permission to manage the Facebook page. Here is an article that may help you to grant the correct permission:

- 
[Confirm the correct level of access for your Facebook page](#/article/troubleshooting-confirming-correct-level-of-access-for-your-facebook-page)

If you have the correct level of permission, then I would recommend you delete the Bizntech Social integration from Facebook and start the process again. Sometimes the integration may fail and needs to be restarted. Here is an article that can help you with that process:

- 
[Delete Bizntech Social Integration from Facebook](#/article/how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook)

## Instagram Issues

First, make sure you don't have any issues with your Facebook page access level. If you can connect your Facebook page to Bizntech Social that means that you have the correct level of permission. Meta requires all Instagram accounts for the following two things:

- 
It should be a Business/professional account

- 
The business account must be connected to a Facebook page.

Here are some articles that might help you with the connection:

- 
[Convert Your Instagram Profile to a Business Profile](#/article/step-by-step-guide-how-to-convert-your-instagram-profile-to-a-business-profile)

- 
[Add a Facebook Page to Your Professional Instagram Account](#/article/how-to-add-a-facebook-page-to-your-professional-instagram-account)

### Your Instagram account still doesn't appear on the list of options.

I recommend deleting the Bizntech Social integration directly from **Facebook**—**not Instagram**. Since Facebook and Instagram are linked under the same Facebook page, integration issues can sometimes occur and may need a reset.

Here is an article that can help you with that process:

- 
[Delete Bizntech Social Integration from Facebook](#/article/how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook)

#### Hopefully, that resolves all the issues you're facing! 💪

# Versión en Español

Es importante entender que tanto la página de Facebook como la cuenta profesional de Instagram están vinculadas a la misma cuenta **personal de Facebook**. Por eso, cuando inicias sesión por primera vez en Instagram o Facebook, te pide que conectes tu cuenta **personal de Facebook**.

## **Problemas con Facebook**

Si no eres el propietario de la página de Facebook, asegúrate de tener el nivel de permiso correcto para gestionar la página de Facebook. Aquí hay un artículo que puede ayudarte a otorgar el permiso corrector:

- 
[Confirma el nivel de acceso correcto para tu página de Facebook](#/article/troubleshooting-confirming-correct-level-of-access-for-your-facebook-page)

Si tienes el nivel de permiso correcto, te recomendaría eliminar la integración de Bizntech Social de Facebook y comenzar el proceso de nuevo. A veces, la integración puede fallar y necesita ser reiniciada. Aquí hay un artículo que puede ayudarte con ese process:

- 
[Elimina la integración de Bizntech Social de Facebook](#/article/how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook)

## **Problemas con Instagram**

Primero, asegúrate de no tener problemas con el nivel de acceso a tu página de Facebook. Si puedes conectar tu página de Facebook a Bizntech Social, eso significa que tienes el nivel de permiso correcto. Meta requiere que todas las cuentas de Instagram cumplan con las siguientes dos condiciones:

- 
Debe ser una cuenta de negocio/profesional.

- 
La cuenta de negocio debe estar conectada a una página de Facebook.
​

Aquí hay algunos artículos que podrían ayudarte con la conexión:

- 
[Convierte tu perfil de Instagram en un perfil de negocio](#/article/step-by-step-guide-how-to-convert-your-instagram-profile-to-a-business-profile)

- 
[Agrega una página de Facebook a tu cuenta profesional de Instagram](#/article/how-to-add-a-facebook-page-to-your-professional-instagram-account)

​

### Si tu cuenta de Instagram aún no aparece en la lista de opciones

Te recomiendo eliminar la integración de Bizntech Social directamente desde **Facebook, no desde Instagram**. Dado que Facebook e Instagram están conectados bajo la misma página de Facebook, a veces pueden ocurrir problemas de integración que requieren un reinicio.

Aquí hay un artículo que puede ayudarte con ese proceso:

- 
[Elimina la integración de Bizntech Social de Facebook](#/article/how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook)

#### ¡Espero que eso resuelva todos los problemas que estás enfrentando! 💪

# CATEGORY: Ai Playground

## How to Use the "Ask AI to Write" Functionality
**Category**: Ai Playground
**Reference ID**: how-to-use-the-ask-ai-to-write-functionality
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
The "Ask AI to Write" functionality in Bizntech Social allows you to effortlessly generate engaging and tailored content for your social media platforms. By providing clear instructions and guidance to our AI-powered system, you can create compelling posts that resonate with your audience. This article will walk you through the step-by-step process of using this powerful feature.

### Step 0: 

Choose the Social Network Before getting started, ensure that you have correctly integrated your desired social network. You can choose the network from the sidebar on the left. If you encounter any integration issues, refer to our "Integrations" article for assistance.

*Example: Choose "LinkedIn" as the social network for which you want to create the content.*

### Step 1:

 Define Your Topic In this initial step, provide a brief description of what you want to write about. Clearly communicate your topic to the AI system, allowing it to understand your intentions and generate relevant content.

*Example: "Engaging Tips for Boosting Social Media Engagement."*

### Step 2: 

Outline the Key Points In the second step, outline the main points you want to include in your content. Provide detailed instructions, important details, specific instructions, data, or any other relevant information that will guide the AI's content creation process.

*Example: *

- 
* Start with an attention-grabbing hook.*

- 
*Highlight the importance of creating valuable content.*

- 
*Share practical strategies for increasing engagement, such as using relevant hashtags and engaging with your audience.*

- 
*Emphasize the power of visual content and storytelling.*

- 
*Conclude with a call to action, encouraging users to like, comment, and share.*

### Step 3:

 Customize Your Copy Here, you have the option to customize the tone and style of your content. Choose a shade that aligns with your desired tone, such as humorous, formal, friendly, or informal. Additionally, select a format that best suits your content, such as discounts/rebates, practical guides, customer recommendations, breaking news, or featuring a product. Finally, choose a Call To Action (CTA) if desired, including options like "Download now," "Link to website," "Like the post," "Comment on the post," or "Tag a friend."

*Example:*

- 
*Tone: Friendly*

- 
*Format: How to Guides*

- 
*Call To Action (CTA): "Tag a friend"*

[*](assets/images/media_68.png)
### 
Step 4: 

Add/Lock Prompt and Create Another After reviewing the input, you can choose to "add" the prompt. This ensures that the AI doesn't make any further changes to the current copy.

However, if you wish to create additional copies based on the same or a different prompt, you can select the "Add" option. This allows you to input a new prompt and receive multiple copies at once, saving you time and effort.

Experiment with different variations and prompts to explore the full potential of the AI-powered content generation in Bizntech Social.

By utilizing the "Add" functionality, you can unlock a range of creative possibilities and receive multiple copies tailored to your needs, all in one go.

Example: 
​
Step 1: Define Your Topic "Creating a Healthy Work-Life Balance"

Step 2: Outline the Key Points Key Points:

- 
Introduction: Importance of work-life balance for overall well-being and productivity.

- 
Tips for Setting Boundaries: Establishing clear boundaries between work and personal life.

- 
Time Management Strategies: Efficiently managing tasks and prioritizing self-care.

- 
Benefits of Work-Life Balance: Improved mental health, increased job satisfaction, and better relationships.

- 
Inspiring Examples: Sharing stories or quotes from successful individuals who have achieved work-life balance.

Step 3: Customize Your Copy Customization:

- 
Tone: Scientific

- 
Format: Industry Tips and Tricks

- 
Call To Action (CTA): "Like the Post"
​
​
[](assets/images/media_69.png)
### 

Step 5: 

Generate Prompts and Go to the Editor Once you've provided all the necessary information in the previous steps, click on the "Generate Prompts" button. Bizntech Social's AI will now generate multiple copies based on your inputs. You'll be directed to the Editor, where you can review and refine the generated copies.

In the Editor, you have full control over the copies. You can make edits to enhance the content, such as adding more specific details, translating the copies into different languages, making them more concise, or even introducing new topics. Feel free to experiment and fine-tune the copies to align them perfectly with your desired social media message.

Remember, the Editor allows you to be creative and customize the generated content to suit your unique needs and preferences. Once you're satisfied with the final versions, you can proceed to publish or schedule the copies directly from the Editor, streamlining your social media content creation process.

With Bizntech Social's "Ask AI to Write" functionality and the powerful Editor, you'll have an efficient and flexible workflow for creating engaging social media content that resonates with your audience.

[](assets/images/media_70.png)

​

By following these simple steps, you can guide the AI to generate content that aligns with your vision and captivates your audience. Explore the endless possibilities of AI-powered content creation and elevate your social media strategy with Bizntech Social.*

​
​
​

## Introducing "Create from Images"
**Category**: Ai Playground
**Reference ID**: introducing-create-from-images
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
We're excited to introduce our latest feature, "Create from Images," designed to empower you with even more creative possibilities for your social media content. With this feature, you can transform your images into engaging, AI-generated copy for various social media platforms effortlessly. Let's dive into how it works:

[](assets/images/media_71.png)

​

**Step 1: Access the Playground**

To get started, head over to our Playground, your creative hub within Bizntech Social. Here, you can craft new documents and access a range of innovative tools.

**Step 2: Select "Create from Images"**

Within the Playground, choose the "Create from Images" functionality. This powerful tool opens up a world of opportunities for content creation.

**Step 3: Define Your Social Media Platform**

Select the social media platform for which you want to create content. Whether it's Facebook, Twitter, Instagram, or another, Bizntech Social has you covered.

**Step 4: Set the Tone**

In the same modal, you have the option to define the tone of your content. Tailor it to your brand's voice, whether you prefer something casual, formal, or somewhere in between.

**Step 5: Choose the Format and Call to Action (CTA)**

Customize your content further by selecting the format you desire, from short and snappy to long-form. You can also choose whether to include a call to action, making your content not only engaging but actionable.

**Step 6: Upload Your Images**

Now comes the creative part. Start uploading your images. Feel free to upload multiple images, knowing that Bizntech Social will generate content based on your selected prompts for each image.

**Step 7: Review and Edit**

Once Bizntech Social has worked its magic, you'll see the AI-generated copies. Review and edit them to align perfectly with your brand's message and style. You have full control to enhance and refine the content as needed.

**Step 8: Schedule Your Content**

With your content perfected, you're now ready to schedule it for publishing. Enjoy the peace of mind knowing that your social media strategy is in capable hands.
​
​

## AI Video Examples
**Category**: Ai Playground
**Reference ID**: ai-video-examples
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
With the new **AI Studio** in Bizntech Social, you can now take your content creation to the next level and generate **videos with Artificial Intelligence**. This feature is designed for you to experiment, try different styles, and let your creativity run wild.

Inside AI Studio, you’ll find **[several AI models](#/article/difference-between-ai-video-models)**, each with its own unique style and output. Some focus on realistic scenes, others lean towards artistic or animated looks. The key is to **test them all** and discover which one best matches the message you want to share.

You can also **upload a reference image** for the AI to use as inspiration. This is perfect if you want to bring a logo to life, animate a character, or turn a still photo into a dynamic short scene.

One important tip: **be as specific as possible with your prompt**. The more details you give (style, environment, colors, actions, emotions), the closer the AI will get to what you have in mind.

Keep in mind that this technology is still very new and constantly evolving. It’s normal if your first attempts don’t come out exactly as imagined — but that’s part of the fun: **experimenting, iterating, and discovering new possibilities**.

With Bizntech Social, you can create **multiple short clips** and later combine them in an external editor to produce longer videos with different scenes. You can also choose whether to have **AI-generated sound** or export the video in silence and add your own voice or music afterward.

The possibilities are endless:

- 
Create **promotional videos** for your products or services.

- 
Develop **storytelling pieces** for social media.

- 
Generate **fun or creative scenes** to grab your audience’s attention.

- 
Animate **illustrations, logos, or characters** to bring them to life.

- 
Produce **experimental or artistic content** that makes your brand stand out.

In short, Bizntech Social’s AI Studio opens up a whole new world of visual creativity for you to explore, experiment, and find fresh ways to connect with your audience.

👉 Here are some real examples created by our users to inspire you.

## How to Use Creative Studio
**Category**: Ai Playground
**Reference ID**: how-to-use-creative-studio
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
**Getting started**

**Open Creative Studio from the left sidebar under AI Images → Creative Studio.**

**Step 1 — Describe your creative**

**In the text area at the top, write a detailed description of what you want to generate. Be specific about the style, mood, and content you’re looking for.**

**Tips for better results:**

- 
Mention the topic, audience, and platform (e.g. “A bold carousel about productivity tips for entrepreneurs”).

- 
Include visual preferences like “vibrant gradients”, “clean typography”, or “minimalist layout”.

- 
The more detail you provide, the closer the result will match your vision.

**Step 2 — Upload a reference image (optional)**

**If you have a product photo or visual asset you want included, drag it into the upload area or click click to browse. Accepted formats: PNG, JPG, WEBP.**

**This is entirely optional. If you skip it, Creative Studio will generate the visual from scratch based on your description.**

**Step 3 — Choose a content type**

**Select one of three options:**

- 
**AI Decides** — lets the AI determine the best format.

- 
**Single Creative** — generates one standalone image.

- 
**Carousel** — generates a multi-slide set. Use the –/+ control to choose between 3 and 10 slides.

**Step 4 — Select an aspect ratio**

**Pick the ratio that matches your target platform:**

- 
**1:1** — Instagram feed, Facebook, LinkedIn.

- 
**4:5** — Instagram portrait posts.

- 
**9:16** — Stories, Reels, TikTok.

- 
**16:9** — YouTube thumbnails, landscape posts.

- 
**2:3** — Pinterest pins.

**Step 5 — Pick a visual style (optional)**

**Choose from a library of agency-grade styles. Click the style selector to browse options. Available styles include:**

- 
**Minimalist Clean** — white space with thin typography.

- 
**Bold Typography** — oversized, attention-grabbing text.

- 
**Gradient / Vibrant** — vivid gradient backgrounds.

- 
**Bold Hook 3D** — punchy 3D elements on pastel covers.

- 
**Bold Statement** — solid-color backgrounds with mixed fonts.

- 
**Glassmorphism** — frosted glass panels with translucent blur.

- 
**Neo-Brutalism** — raw geometric shapes with thick outlines.

- 
**AI Decides** — lets the AI choose the most fitting style for your prompt.

You can also use the search bar at the top of the style selector to find a specific style quickly.

**Step 6 — Add brand context (optional)**

**Toggle any combination of these options to keep your creatives on brand:**

- 
**Include brand context** — applies your brand’s identity and messaging.

- 
**Include brand logo** — places your logo on every generated image.

- 
**Use brand colors** — applies your brand’s color palette to the design.

Brand settings are pulled from your **Company Settings** in Bizntech Social. Make sure your brand identity is configured there before using this feature.

**Generating your creative**

**Once you’ve completed the steps above, click the Generate button at the bottom. The credit cost is displayed next to the button (e.g. “25 credits” for a single creative or “175 credits” for a 7-slide carousel).**

**Your results will appear on the Canvas — the preview area on the right side of the screen. Generation usually takes a few seconds.**

**Working with the Canvas**

**The Canvas is your design workspace where all generated creatives appear. You can zoom in and out using the controls at the bottom (or scroll), and navigate between slides in a carousel by scrolling horizontally.**

**From the Canvas toolbar you can:**

- 
**Save to Media** — sends all images to your Media Library for future use.

- 
**Download All** — downloads every image in the set to your device.

- 
**Use in Post** — opens the Post Composer with your creatives attached, ready to publish to any social network connected to your Bizntech Social account.

- 
**Create New** — starts a fresh generation from scratch.

**Editing individual images with AI**

**Click on any generated image to select it. An Edit Instructions panel will appear where you can type a natural-language instruction — for example:**

- 
“Change color to violet”

- 
“Make the text larger”

- 
“Replace the background with a sunset”

- 
“Remove the logo”

Click **Edit Image** to apply the change. The AI will regenerate that specific image while preserving the rest of your set.

You can also delete any image you don’t want and keep the rest.

**Credit usage**

**Creative Studio uses Media Credits from your Bizntech Social plan. The exact cost depends on the content type and number of images:**

- 
A single creative costs approximately 25 credits.

- 
A 7-slide carousel costs approximately 175 credits.

- 
Editing an existing image also consumes credits.

You can check your remaining credits in the **Media Credits** section in the left sidebar.

**Frequently asked questions**

**Can I edit a creative after generating it?**

**Yes. Click on any image and use the Edit Instructions panel to modify it with a text prompt. The AI will regenerate that specific image.**

**Which social networks can I publish to?**

**All networks connected to your Bizntech Social account via API. This includes Instagram, Facebook, LinkedIn, X (Twitter), TikTok, YouTube, and more.**

**Do I need to set up my brand before using Creative Studio?**

**No, brand context is optional. However, for consistent branding across all creatives, we recommend configuring your brand identity, logo and colors in Company Settings first.**

**What’s the maximum number of slides in a carousel?**

**You can generate between 3 and 10 slides per carousel.**

**Can I choose an AI model?**

**Yes. You can switch the AI model from the top navigation bar. Creative Studio works with the model selected in your workspace settings.**

# CATEGORY: Calendar

## Creating and scheduling posts using Bizntech Social’s Calendar
**Category**: Calendar
**Reference ID**: creating-and-scheduling-posts-using-bizntech-social-s-calendar
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

Creating high-quality content is only half the battle when it comes to social media marketing. Posting your content at the right time and on the right platform is also crucial for gaining maximum engagement and reaching your target audience. Bizntech Social's post scheduling and automation feature helps you streamline this process and ensures that your content is posted at the optimal time for maximum impact.

## How to Schedule Posts

Once you've created your content in Bizntech Social's Ai Playground, you can easily schedule it for posting using Bizntech Social's calendar feature. The calendar displays all your scheduled posts in a user-friendly interface, organized by dates, allowing you to see and manage all your posts at a glance.

To schedule a post, simply drag and drop your content into the desired date and time slot on the calendar. Bizntech Social will prompt you to confirm the post before it is scheduled, giving you the option to edit or delete the post if needed.

[](assets/images/media_73.png)

## Automating Post Scheduling

In addition to scheduling posts manually, Bizntech Social also offers an automated post scheduling feature. By analyzing the performance of your social media accounts, Bizntech Social is able to determine the best times to post your content for maximum engagement and reach.

To automate post scheduling, simply select the social media platform you want to post to, and Bizntech Social will automatically generate a posting schedule based on your account's performance data. This feature saves you time and ensures that your content is posted at the optimal time for the best engagement.

[](assets/images/media_74.png)

## Using Media Content to Enhance Your Posts

To enhance the quality of your posts, Bizntech Social provides a variety of media content, including images, videos, and gifs related to your brand or product. You can easily access these media files within the calendar interface, making it easy to add engaging media content to your posts.

In addition to Bizntech Social's media content, you can also import images and videos from Google Drive or Dropbox, allowing you to use custom designs created by your team to accompany your written content.

[](assets/images/media_75.png)

## Conclusion

Bizntech Social's post scheduling and  automation feature is a powerful tool that streamlines the content creation process, ensures that your content is posted at the optimal time, and provides a variety of media content to enhance your posts. By integrating with all your preferred social media platforms, Bizntech Social makes it easy to implement an effective social media marketing strategy.

# Versión en Español

Crear contenido de alta calidad es solo la mitad de la batalla cuando se trata de marketing en redes sociales. Publicar tu contenido en el momento adecuado y en la plataforma correcta también es crucial para obtener el máximo compromiso y alcanzar a tu público objetivo. La función de programación y automatización de publicaciones de Bizntech Social te ayuda a simplificar este proceso y garantiza que tu contenido se publique en el momento óptimo para lograr el mayor impacto.

## **Cómo Programar Publicaciones**

Una vez que hayas creado tu contenido en el AI Playground de Bizntech Social, puedes programarlo fácilmente para su publicación utilizando la función de calendario de Bizntech Social. El calendario muestra todas tus publicaciones programadas en una interfaz fácil de usar, organizada por fechas, lo que te permite ver y gestionar todas tus publicaciones de un vistazo.

Para programar una publicación, simplemente arrastra y suelta tu contenido en el espacio de fecha y hora deseado en el calendario. Bizntech Social te pedirá que confirmes la publicación antes de que se programe, dándote la opción de editarla o eliminarla si es necesario.
[](assets/images/media_76.png)

## **Automatización de la Programación de Publicaciones**

Además de programar publicaciones manualmente, Bizntech Social también ofrece una función de programación automática. Al analizar el rendimiento de tus cuentas de redes sociales, Bizntech Social es capaz de determinar los mejores momentos para publicar tu contenido y lograr el mayor compromiso y alcance.

Para automatizar la programación de publicaciones, simplemente selecciona la plataforma de redes sociales a la que deseas publicar, y Bizntech Social generará automáticamente un horario de publicación basado en los datos de rendimiento de tu cuenta. Esta función te ahorra tiempo y asegura que tu contenido se publique en el momento óptimo para obtener el mejor compromise.
[](assets/images/media_77.png)

## **Usar Contenido Multimedia para Mejorar Tus Publicaciones**

Para mejorar la calidad de tus publicaciones, Bizntech Social ofrece una variedad de contenido multimedia, incluidos imágenes, videos y gifs relacionados con tu marca o producto. Puedes acceder fácilmente a estos archivos multimedia dentro de la interfaz del calendario, lo que facilita agregar contenido multimedia atractivo a tus publicaciones.

Además del contenido multimedia de Bizntech Social, también puedes importar imágenes y videos desde Google Drive o Dropbox, lo que te permite utilizar diseños personalizados creados por tu equipo para acompañar tu contenido escrito.
[](assets/images/media_78.png)

## **Conclusión**

La función de programación y automatización de publicaciones de Bizntech Social es una herramienta poderosa que simplifica el proceso de creación de contenido, asegura que tu contenido se publique en el momento óptimo, y proporciona una variedad de contenido multimedia para mejorar tus publicaciones. Al integrarse con todas tus plataformas de redes sociales preferidas, Bizntech Social facilita la implementación de una estrategia de marketing en redes sociales efectiva.
