## How to Fix Error #200 on Facebook
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
Languages available in this article:

- 
[English](#h_e8e670b7e8)

- 
[Español](#h_7c6dddfc5f)

# English Version

## **These are Common Error #200 Messages you might see:**

- 
Insufficient permission to post to target on behalf of the viewer

- 
Requires either publish_actions permission or manage_pages and publish_pages as an admin with sufficient administrative permission

- 
The subject does not have permission to post videos on this target.

- 
The subject does not have permission to post photos on this page.

- 
Requires either publish_to_groups permission and app being installed in the group, or manage_pages and publish_pages as an admin with sufficient administrative permission.

*This error usually means that the Bizntech Social web app has not been granted full permission to publish to your Facebook Group or Page.*

## 

*
- 
Login to your Facebook.com account, and navigate to **Settings & privacy**

- 
Under Settings & privacy, click  **Settings **and** **choose **Business Integrations **from the left panel

- 
Search for the **Bizntech Social **application in the Business Integrations. Once you find the application, click on **Remove**

- 
Upon clicking the remove button, a confirmation popup will appear on the screen. Check/Uncheck the notification** checkbox **depending on your preference, then click on **Remove**

- 
**Bizntech Social **has been successfully removed from integrations.

### In Bizntech Social:

Now, open Bizntech Social to restart the connection process and ensure that there are no problems. The following steps will guide you through the process.

- 
Log in to your Bizntech Social account, and go to **Company Settings > [Integrations](https://bizntechstl.com)**

- 
Look for the Facebook integration and press the **trash icon**

- 
**Confirm** the action.
[](assets/images/media_309.png)
- 
After successfully deleting the integration, go under the **Not Integrated** section and search for the Facebook option. Click on **Integrate Facebook**

- 
A new pop-up will appear. Click on the **Connect with Facebook** button.

- 
After clicking, a new website will appear asking you to approve the permission asked by Bizntech Social. It is important to** approve all permissions** to avoid errors while using the platform in the future. 

- 
After accepting the permissions, you will be directed to the Bizntech Social website. There, you will see a list of Facebook pages connected to your logged-in account. If your desired page does not appear, make sure you are logged in to the correct account. If needed, please repeat all steps outlined in this article.

- 
Select the Facebook page you would like to use with Bizntech Social and click on **Connect Facebook Page**
[](assets/images/media_310.png)
### Hopefully, that resolved all the issues you're facing! 

# Versión en Español

Estas son las posibles Mensajes de Error Común #200 que podrías ver:

- 
Permiso insuficiente para publicar en nombre del espectador.

- 
Requiere permiso de **publish_actions** o **manage_pages** y **publish_pages** como administrador con permisos administrativos suficientes.

- 
La persona no tiene permiso para publicar videos en este objetivo.

- 
La persona no tiene permiso para publicar fotos en esta página.

- 
Requiere el permiso **publish_to_groups** y que la aplicación esté instalada en el grupo, o **manage_pages** y **publish_pages** como administrador con permisos administrativos suficientes.

Este error generalmente significa que la aplicación web de Bizntech Social no ha recibido los permisos completos para publicar en tu Grupo o Página de Facebook.*

## Paso a paso

### En Facebook

- 
 Inicia sesión en tu cuenta de Facebook.com y navega a "**Configuración y privacidad**".

- 
En "Configuración y privacidad", haz clic en "**Configuración**". Luego, selecciona "**Integraciones comerciales**" en el panel Izquierdo.

- 
Busca la aplicación **Bizntech Social** en la sección de "Integraciones comerciales". Una vez que encuentres la aplicación, haz clic en "**Eliminar**".

- 
Al hacer clic en el botón de eliminar, aparecerá una ventana emergente de confirmación en la pantalla. Marca o desmarca la casilla de notificación según tu preferencia, luego haz clic en "**Eliminar**".

- 
¡Listo! **Bizntech Social** se ha eliminado con éxito de las integraciones.

### En Bizntech Social

Ahora, abre tu cuenta de Bizntech Social para reiniciar el proceso de conexión y asegurarte de que no haya problemas. Los siguientes pasos te guiarán a través del proceso.

- 
Inicia sesión en tu cuenta de Bizntech Social y ve a **Configuración de la Compañía > Integraciones**.

- 
Busca la integración de **Facebook** y presiona el icono de la papelera.

- 
**Confirma** la acción para eliminar la integración y luego podrás volver a configurarla correctamente.

- 
Después de eliminar exitosamente la integración ve a la sección** No Integrado** y busca la opción de Facebook y haz clic en **Integrar Facebook**.

- 
Aparecerá una ventana emergente. Haz clic en el botón **Conectar con Facebook**.

- 
Se abrirá un sitio web de Facebook solicitando los permisos necesarios para vincular Bizntech Social. Es importante **aprobar todos los permisos** para evitar errores en el futuro al usar la plataforma.

- 
Después de aceptar los permisos, serás redirigido de nuevo al sitio web de Bizntech Social, donde verás una lista de las páginas de Facebook conectadas a la cuenta que has iniciado sesión. Si la página que deseas no aparece, asegúrate de estar conectado a la cuenta correcta. Si es necesario, repite los pasos anteriores.

- 
Selecciona la página de Facebook que te gustaría utilizar con Bizntech Social y haz clic en **Conectar página de Facebook** para completar el proceso. 
[](assets/images/media_317.png)
### ¡Espero que eso haya resuelto todos los problemas que estás enfrentando!

## Troubleshooting Analytics: "Failed to Execute 'Setitem' 'Storage'" Error
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: troubleshooting-analytics-failed-to-execute-setitem-storage-error
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
English Version🇺🇸

[](assets/images/media_318.jpeg)

If you encounter the error message "Failed to execute 'setitem' 'storage'" in the analytics section of Bizntech Social, don't worry, there's a simple solution to resolve this issue. This error typically occurs due to stored cookies on your browser.

Here's how you can fix it:
​

- 
Open Chrome or any other browser on your computer.

- 
In the top right corner, click on **More More** (three dots) and then select **Clear browsing data**.

- 
Choose a time range (for example, "Last hour" or "All time").

- 
Select the types of information you want to delete.

- 
Click on **Clear data**.
​
​
An easier way to find this is here: Just Click in the URL 
[](assets/images/media_319.jpeg)

Spanish Version 🇪🇸

# 
​**Solución de Problemas en Analytics: Error "Failed to Execute 'Setitem' 'Storage'"**

Si te aparece el mensaje de error "Failed to execute 'setitem' 'storage'" en la sección de analytics de Bizntech Social, no te preocupes, hay una solución simple para resolver este problema. Este error generalmente ocurre debido a cookies almacenadas en tu navegador.
[](assets/images/media_320.jpeg)
Sigue estos pasos para solucionarlo:

- 
Abre Chrome u otro navegador en tu computadora.

- 
En la esquina superior derecha, haz clic en "Más" (tres puntos) y selecciona "Borrar datos de navegación".

- 
Elige un rango de tiempo (por ejemplo, "Última hora" o "Todo el tiempo").

- 
Selecciona los tipos de información que deseas eliminar.

- 
Haz clic en "Borrar datos".

**Un Acceso Más Rápido**
Para facilitarte aún más, puedes acceder directamente escribiendo en la barra de URL:
​chrome://settings/clearBrowserData

Con esto, deberías resolver el problema y poder acceder a las analytics sin inconvenientes.
​
​

## Error from Instagram: (#10) The user is not an Instagram Business
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: error-from-instagram-10-the-user-is-not-an-instagram-business
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

There are two different types of Instagram professional accounts:

- 
Professional Business

- 
Professional Creator

**Because of the restrictions imposed by Instagram's API**, attempting to post an Instagram Story to a Creator account will result in an error message stating: Error from Instagram: (#10) The user is not an Instagram Business. This is because only **Professional Business** accounts have the capability to directly post Instagram Stories.

## How to switch your account to a Professional Business?

- 
Visit your [Instagram page](https://instagram.com) on your web browser. 

- 
Click on **Profile** located on the left-hand side of the screen, then choose the **Settings symbol.**
[](assets/images/media_322.png)

- 
Go to **Settings and Privacy.**
[](assets/images/media_323.png)

- 
If you currently possess a Professional account you will see under the "For professionals" section on the left sidebar the option "**Creator tools and controls**".

If your Account is a Personal account you will need to switch to a professional account. Here is an article that can help you with that: [Convert your personal account into a Business profile](https://app.intercom.com/a/apps/p63otiu3/articles/articles/7915452/show) 
​

- 
Click on **"Switch to business account". **
[](assets/images/media_324.png)

- 
Click on **"Switch". **
[](assets/images/media_325.png)
# Versión en Español

Hay dos tipos diferentes de cuentas profesionales de Instagram:

- 
Cuenta de Empresa Profesional

- 
Cuenta de Creador Profesional

Debido a las restricciones impuestas por la API de Instagram, intentar publicar una historia de Instagram en una cuenta de Creador resultará en un mensaje de error que dice: Error de Instagram: (#10) The user is not an Instagram Business. Esto se debe a que solo las cuentas de Empresa Profesional tienen la capacidad de publicar historias de Instagram directamente.
​

**¿Cómo cambiar tu cuenta a una Cuenta de Empresa Profesional?**

- 
Visita tu [página de Instagram](https://instagram.com) en tu navegador web.

- 
Haz clic en **Perfil** ubicado en el lado izquierdo de la pantalla y luego elige el **símbolo de Configuración**. 
[](assets/images/media_326.png)

- 
Ve a** Configuración y privacidad. **
[](assets/images/media_327.png)

- 
Si actualmente posees una cuenta Profesional, verás en la sección "Para profesionales" en la barra lateral izquierda la opción "**Herramientas y controles para creadores**"

Si tu cuenta es una cuenta Personal, necesitarás cambiar a una cuenta profesional. Aquí hay un artículo que puede ayudarte con eso: [Convierte tu cuenta personal en un perfil de negocio.](https://app.intercom.com/a/apps/p63otiu3/articles/articles/7915452/show)
​

- 
Haz clic en "**Cambiar a cuenta de empresa**".
[](assets/images/media_328.png)

- 
**Haz clic en "Cambiar". **
[](assets/images/media_329.png)Related Articles

## My screen is showing up gray
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: my-screen-is-showing-up-gray
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

It is not very common to happen, but if eventually when you want to enter Bizntech Social, your screen shows gray in full as in the picture below, with no possibility to navigate to any other site within Bizntech Social, it is most likely that your browser is not being able to correctly update the latest Bizntech Social updates.
[](assets/images/media_330.png)
Most of the time, it's just a matter of waiting until your browser automatically updates, but in order to fix it quickly it's as simple as deleting your cookies. Here's how you can do it:

## Deleting cookies

- 
Go to [https://bizntechstl.com class="intercom-interblocks-paragraph no-margin intercom-interblocks-align-left">On the left of the URL box, you will see a padlock icon (or a similar icon ).

Click on that icon and select where it says **cookies** 

- 
Then go to where it says **Manage cookies**

- 
Delete those cookies from app.Bizntech Social

- 
Refresh the website
](https://bizntechstl.com)

**Done! You should now be able to see all Bizntech Social features once again.**

## 
Alternative methods

If you don't want to delete your cookies, other options can be:

- 
Using another browser

- 
Using Bizntech Social through an incognito tab.

In case you still need help with your account don't hesitate to contact the support team. We are hear for you 💪

- 
Luego ve a donde dice **Administrar cookies**.

- 
Elimina esas cookies de **app.Bizntech Social**.

- 
Refresca el sitio web.
](https://bizntechstl.com)

**¡Listo! Ahora deberías poder ver todas las funciones de Bizntech Social nuevamente.**

## Métodos alternativos

Si no deseas eliminar tus cookies, otras opciones pueden ser:

- 
Usar otro navegador.

- 
Utilizar Bizntech Social a través de una pestaña de incógnito.

En caso de que aún necesites ayuda con tu cuenta, no dudes en contactar al equipo de soporte. Estamos aquí para ayudarte 💪.

## Troubleshooting Medium
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: troubleshooting-medium
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# Troubleshooting Medium Integration with Bizntech Social

If you're experiencing issues with scheduling posts on Medium through Bizntech Social, the problem is likely due to the API token needing renewal. Follow these steps to reintegrate Medium with a new token:

- 
**Disintegrate Medium from Bizntech Social:**

Go to your Bizntech Social account settings.

- 
Navigate to the "Integrations" section.

- 
Find Medium and click on "Disintegrate" to remove the current integration.

- 
**Revoke and Obtain a New Token on Medium:**

Log in to your Medium account.

- 
Go to the "Settings" and Security section.

- 
Locate the API tokens and click on "Revoke token" to invalidate the current token.

- 
Generate a new token and copy it.
[](assets/images/media_336.jpeg)

- 
**Reintegrate Medium with Bizntech Social:**

Return to your Bizntech Social account.

- 
Go back to the "Integrations" section.

- 
Select Medium and click on "Integrate."

- 
Paste the new token from Medium and complete the integration process.

[](assets/images/media_337.jpeg)

This should resolve any issues with scheduling posts on Medium. The need for a new token is due to occasional API failures that require re-establishing the connection.

## Pinterest Errors While Publishing
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: pinterest-errors-while-publishing
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

If you have published a post on Pinterest and got an error. Below you will find the error codes along with a recommendation from our side:

**#**

**Error Message or Code**

**Problem**

**Solution**

**1**

Media upload took too long

The file overcomes some of the limitations set by Pinterest. Check the [article with the limitations](#/article/media-upload-requirement-and-limitations#h_f6a8ed87e9).

Create the file within the parameters required by Pinterest. If the file is already created an alternative could be to use an online video/image converter.

**2**

Sorry! We blocked this link because it may redirect to spam

Pinterest detected that your link redirects to other pages, contains misleading, inappropriate, or spammy content, or otherwise violates their [Terms of Service](https://policy.pinterest.com/terms-of-service) and [Community Guidelines](https://policy.pinterest.com/community-guidelines)

Changing the link. It can happen that although you have changed it, Pinterest continues to block your links for some time after this first event. In that case, upload posts without links for a while.

# Versión en Español

Si has publicado un post en Pinterest y te ha dado error. A continuación encontrará los códigos de error junto con una recomendación de nuestra parte:

**#**

**Mensaje o Código de Error**

**Problema**

**Solución**

**1**

Media upload took too long

El archivo supera algunas de las limitaciones establecidas por Pinterest. [Consulta el artículo con las limitaciones](#/article/media-upload-requirement-and-limitations#h_f6a8ed87e9).

Crea el archivo dentro de los parámetros requeridos por Pinterest. Si el archivo ya está creado, una alternativa podría ser usar un convertidor de video/imagen en línea.

**2**

Sorry! We blocked this link because it may redirect to spam

Pinterest detectó que tu enlace redirige a otras páginas, contiene contenido engañoso, inapropiado o de spam, o viola sus [Términos de Servicio](https://policy.pinterest.com/terms-of-service) y [Directrices de la Comunidad](https://policy.pinterest.com/community-guidelines)

Cambiar el enlace. Puede suceder que, aunque lo hayas cambiado, Pinterest siga bloqueando tus enlaces durante un tiempo después de este primer incidente. En ese caso, sube publicaciones sin enlaces por un tiempo.

## Facebook Errors While Publishing
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: facebook-errors-while-publishing
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

If you are having problems connecting to Facebook, I recommend that you first read the following article with common errors when making the connection:

[Facebook Integration Issues](#/article/resolving-instagram-and-facebook-integration-issues)

If you have published a post on Facebook and got an error. Below you will find the error codes provided by Meta along with a recommendation from our side:

**#**

**Error Message or Code**

**Problem**

**Solution**

1

Your access token has expired or has been invalidated

This error usually results when we have lost your profile connection and need you to reconnect it to Bizntech Social. Most social networks need you to re-authorize applications from third parties (like Bizntech Social) every 2-3 months or so, to make sure you can use them to publish them on your profile.

This can also happen if you have changed your account password.

Try [reconnecting your social network](https://docs.contentstudio.io/article/669-how-to-refresh-token-expiry) to renew token - accept all permissions, sign in, and once renewed, try to publish again.

2

OAuthException: (#32) Page request limited reached (code 32, subcode null)

The API call limit for your page was exceeded. It's focused on daily active persons (DAP): the higher the DAP on your domain, the higher the level of the rate cap.

Once the threshold is reached, Facebook locks the Page and the only way to get this "resolved" is to wait until the Page is unlocked again.

3

OAuthException: Invalid parameter (code 100, subcode 2061006)

Mostly, this occurs when a URL is not in the right format.

Check the field where the URL link is pasted and correct the URL. Give it another try.

4

OAuthException: Invalid parameter (code 100, subcode 1349125)

This error appears when the image that you're trying to upload is either corrupted or in a format that we do not support. 
​

Make sure the image is either JPG, PNG, and is not more than 8 MB. Also, ascertain that your file is not corrupted. 

If you have downloaded the image from another website, make sure the download is complete before uploading it! 

5

Your account has been blocked (code 368, subcode 1390008)

It looks like you were misusing this feature by going too fast. You've been blocked from using it.

 Please refrain from posting "spam" or identical pictures. Facebook blocks the content and the page both. If your account has been suspended contact with Facebook.com

6

Facebook: (#10) This endpoint requires the 'pages_read_engagement' permission or the 'Page Public Content Access' feature or the 'Page Public Metadata Access' feature

The user did not accept the necessary permissions.

Remove the integration from facebook.com and integrate Instagram once again accepting ALL permissions. See this [step-by-step on how to remove the integration from facebook.com](#/article/how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook)

7

App\Jobs\PublishPost has been attempted too many times or run too long

Facebook couldn't upload your post. No particular reason was specified.

This error is reported by Facebook when they do not process the request to publish.
Normally this error is due to an overload on their servers.

Try uploading the post once again. If the error persists try duplicating the post or uploading it directly from Instagram (as not all of their servers may be affected)

8

Please reduce the amount of data you’re asking for, then retry your request

This is a known bug on Facebook's end for their API. They have stated that the cause is that their servers run out of memory.

Sometimes the post is published successfully to your Facebook Page, even though Bizntech Social shows the post failed.

You should check your Facebook Page to see if the post was published or not, and take action accordingly.

If retrying the post continues to fail, the Facebook API may be unable to publish the post so you may want to try scheduling the post for a later time

# Versión en Español

Si estás teniendo problemas para conectar con Facebook, te recomiendo que primero leas el siguiente artículo sobre errores comunes al realizar la conexión:

[Problemas de Integración con Facebook](#/article/resolving-instagram-and-facebook-integration-issues)

Si has publicado una publicación en Facebook y obtuviste un error, a continuación encontrarás los códigos de error proporcionados por Meta junto con una recomendación de nuestra parte:

**#**

**Mensaje de error o código**

**Problema**

**Solución**

1

Your access token has expired or has been invalidated

Este error suele ocurrir cuando hemos perdido la conexión con tu perfil y necesitamos que lo reconectes a Bizntech Social. La mayoría de las redes sociales requieren que reautorices las aplicaciones de terceros (como Bizntech Social) cada 2-3 meses aproximadamente, para asegurarte de que puedas usarlas para publicar en tu perfil.

También puede ocurrir si has cambiado la contraseña de tu cuenta.

Intenta [reconectar tu red social](https://docs.contentstudio.io/article/669-how-to-refresh-token-expiry) para renovar el token: acepta todos los permisos, inicia sesión y, una vez renovado, intenta publicar nuevamente.

2

OAuthException: (#32) Page request limited reached (code 32, subcode null)

Se excedió el límite de llamadas API para tu página. Este límite está basado en la cantidad de personas activas diarias (DAP): cuanto mayor sea el DAP en tu dominio, mayor será el nivel del límite de tasa.

Una vez que se alcanza el umbral, Facebook bloquea la página y la única forma de "resolverlo" es esperar hasta que la página se desbloquee nuevamente.

3

OAuthException: Invalid parameter (code 100, subcode 2061006)

Generalmente, esto ocurre cuando una URL no está en el formato correcto.

Revisa el campo donde se pegó el enlace de la URL y corrige la URL. Inténtalo nuevamente.

4

OAuthException: Invalid parameter (code 100, subcode 1349125)

Este error aparece cuando la imagen que intentas subir está rota o en un formato que no es compatible.

Asegúrate de que la imagen esté en formato JPG o PNG y que no pese más de 8 MB. Verifica que el archivo no esté roto. Si has descargado la imagen desde otro sitio web, asegúrate de que la descarga esté completa antes de intentar subirla.

5

Your account has been blocked (code 368, subcode 1390008)

Parece que has estado haciendo un uso indebido de esta función al ir demasiado rápido. Se te ha bloqueado el acceso a esta función.

Por favor, evita publicar "spam" o imágenes idénticas. Facebook bloquea tanto el contenido como la página. Si tu cuenta ha sido suspendida, contacta con Facebook.com.

6

Facebook: (#10) This endpoint requires the 'pages_read_engagement' permission or the 'Page Public Content Access' feature or the 'Page Public Metadata Access' feature

El usuario no aceptó los permisos necesarios.

Elimina la integración desde facebook.com e integra Instagram nuevamente aceptando TODOS los permisos. Consulta este [paso a paso sobre cómo eliminar la integración desde facebook.com.](#/article/how-to-fix-error-200-on-facebook)

7

App\Jobs\PublishPost has been attempted too many times or run too long

Facebook no pudo subir tu publicación. No se especificó un motivo en particular.

Este error es reportado por Facebook cuando no procesan la solicitud de publicación. Normalmente, este error se debe a una sobrecarga en sus servidores.

Intenta subir la publicación nuevamente. Si el error persiste, prueba duplicar la publicación o subirla directamente desde Instagram (ya que no todos sus servidores pueden estar afectados).

8

Please reduce the amount of data you’re asking for, then retry your request

Este es un error conocido del lado de Facebook en su API. Han indicado que la causa es que sus servidores se quedan sin memorial.

A veces, la publicación se publica correctamente en tu Página de Facebook, **aunque Bizntech Social muestre que falló**.

Te recomendamos que revises tu Página de Facebook para verificar si la publicación se realizó o no, y actúes en consecuencia.

Si al volver a intentar sigue fallando, es posible que la API de Facebook no pueda publicar el contenido. En ese caso, podrías probar programar la publicación para un momento posterior.

## How to Verify Your YouTube Account
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: how-to-verify-your-youtube-account
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

YouTube has **three levels of verification**, and the [features available to you](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/9890437) depend on which level you’ve reached. These levels are:

- 
**Standard**

- 
**Intermediate**

- 
**Advanced**

We recommend that at least the Intermediate features be enabled. These features are easy to enable; however, it is just a simple matter of applying for them. Here is how: 

*👉 Please note that these limitations **are not set by Bizntech Social**, but rather by YouTube and the way it applies permissions to users.*

​

### How to enable YouTube's Intermediate and Advanced features

- 
Navigate to [your channel page](https://www.youtube.com/account) and click on "Channel status and features".
[*](assets/images/media_338.png)
- 
You will see a list of available features. Note that in our case, we have all 3 feature groups enabled. 
[](assets/images/media_339.png)
- 
If your Intermediate or Advanced features are not enabled, you can expand and apply for them.
​

**Can you publish without features enabled using Bizntech Social?**** Absolutely. We do, however, recommend that you at least enable intermediate features, as it will allow you to **publish longer videos and customize thumbnails.***

### ⚡ Extra notes

- 
You only need to verify once per account.

- 
If you just verified, it might take a little while before the API lets you use the new limits and capabilities. 

- 
If your account is under restrictions (copyright strikes, community guideline issues), custom thumbnails might still be disabled until the restriction expires.

# Versión en Español

YouTube cuenta con **tres niveles de verificación**, y según el nivel en el que te encuentres, tendrás [acceso a distintas funcionalidades](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/9890437) dentro de la plataforma. Estos niveles son:

- 
**Estándar**

- 
**Intermedio**

- 
**Avanzado**

Recomendamos que al menos las funciones intermedias estén habilitadas. Estas funciones son fáciles de activar; simplemente se trata de solicitarlas. Aquí te mostramos cómo hacerlo:

*👉 Ten en cuenta que estas limitaciones **no dependen de Bizntech Social**, sino de YouTube y de la manera en que aplica los permisos a los usuarios.*

### **Cómo habilitar las funciones intermedias y avanzadas de YouTube**

- 
Ve a la página de [tu canal](https://www.youtube.com/account) y haz clic en **"Estado y funciones del canal"**.
[*](assets/images/media_340.png)
- 
Verás una lista de funciones disponibles. En nuestro caso, tenemos habilitados los 3 grupos de funciones.
[](assets/images/media_341.png)
- 
Si tus funciones intermedias o avanzadas no están habilitadas, puedes expandir la opción y solicitarlas.
​

**¿Puedes publicar en Bizntech Social sin tener habilitadas estas funciones?***
​*¡Absolutamente! Sin embargo, recomendamos que al menos habilites las funciones intermedias, ya que te **permitirán publicar videos más largos y personalizar las miniaturas.***

### ⚡ **Notas adicionales**

- 
Solo necesitas verificar una vez por cuenta.

- 
Si acabas de verificar, puede tardar un poco antes de que la API te permita usar los nuevos límites y capacidades.

- 
Si tu cuenta está bajo restricciones (por ejemplo, advertencias de copyright o problemas con las normas de la comunidad), las miniaturas personalizadas podrían seguir deshabilitadas hasta que la restricción expire.

## YouTube Errors While Publishing
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: youtube-errors-while-publishing
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
If you have published a post on YouTube and got an error. Below you will find the most common errors provided by YouTube, along with a recommendation from our side:

**#**

**Error**

**Problem**

Solution

**1**

Daily custom thumbnail limit reached

There's a limit to how many custom thumbnails a channel can upload each day. Limits may vary by country/region, channel history and account level

[Verify your account](#/article/how-to-verify-your-youtube-account) to increase your limit or wait 24 hours

**2**

Content is not allowed by YouTube policies

You have uploaded content that is not allowed by [YouTube content polices](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/2801973?hl=en)

You won't be able to upload that video. Make sure to change the content according to [YouTube content polices](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/2801973?hl=en) and [community guidance](https://www.youtube.com/howyoutubeworks/our-policies/)

**3**

The authenticated user doesn't have permissions to upload and set custom video thumbnails. Please verify your account by putting telephone number and enable "customised thumbnails" in your account's panel

You need to verify your account to be able to upload thumbnails 

Verify your account. Check the following guide: 
​[How to verify your YouTube account](#/article/how-to-verify-your-youtube-account)

**4**

Daily upload limit reached

You have a limit of videos that you can upload per day. Limits may vary by country/region, channel history and account level

[Verify your account](#/article/how-to-verify-your-youtube-account) to increase your limit or wait 24 hours.

**5**

We have encountered unexpected problems

YouTube has experienced some momentary problems

Try waiting a few minutes, then upload again

**6**

The server has rejected the file

This error message occurs when you're uploading a file in the wrong format.

Make sure that you're using a [supported file type](https://support.google.com/youtube/troubleshooter/2888402).

**7**

Can't upload. Your channel status or account settings currently do not support uploads

This error message may occur when:

Your channel has a [Community Guidelines strike](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/2802032).

Your admin has [turned on Restricted mode](https://support.google.com/a/answer/6212415?hl=en) on your Google Workspace account.

Check your account to see if you got a strike and talk to the account admin to make sure you have all permissions to manage the YouTube account.

**8**

Rejected (file too small)

This error message occurs when you've tried to upload a file that is smaller than 2 KB

Make sure your video file is at least 2 KB and try to upload again

**9**

Failed (empty .mov file)

This error message occurs when QuickTime movies are saved as a reference movie.

To make sure your video converts properly, select **Save as a self-contained movie** and [try to upload](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/57407) again.

## Why can't I add my YouTube Account?
**Category**: Troubleshooting
**Reference ID**: why-can-t-i-add-my-youtube-account
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

If you’re trying to connect a YouTube channel to Bizntech Social but it’s not appearing in the connection list, don’t worry, this is a common situation. Below, we’ll explain why this happens and how you can resolve it.

### 🔒 Why Your Channel Might Not Be Showing Up

The way Bizntech Social connects to YouTube is through the **official YouTube API**. This is the system that allows external platforms (like Bizntech Social) to securely communicate with YouTube.

Because of YouTube’s API rules:

- 
**Only the actual channel Owner can connect a YouTube channel.**

- 
Users with other roles (like *Manager* or *Editor* in YouTube Studio) are not permitted to authorize these connections.

*👉 This is not a Bizntech Social limitation; it’s a restriction set directly by YouTube to protect accounts and ensure that only the primary owner can grant access.*

### 👩‍💻 What to Do if You’re Not the Channel Owner

If you’re managing a YouTube channel but don’t own it, you won’t be able to connect it to Bizntech Social yourself. Instead, here’s what you can do:

- 
**Ask the channel Owner to log in to Bizntech Social** and connect the YouTube account.

- 
Once the Owner connects the channel, you (as a user or Admin in Bizntech Social) will still be able to schedule, publish content, use AI tools to create posts, review analytics and more.

This way, you can still fully manage the channel through Bizntech Social once it’s connected, even if you weren’t the one to authorize the connection.

### ✅ Next Steps

- 
If you’re unsure who the channel Owner is, check in YouTube Studio under **Settings > Permissions**. Only the person listed as *Owner* can complete the integration.

- 
If you already know who the Owner is, simply invite them to your Bizntech Social workspace as a user and guide them through the connection process. [Learn how to add users to Bizntech Social here](#/article/how-to-invite-a-new-user-to-my-company).

✨ **Tip:** To avoid connection issues, make sure the Owner is signed in with the correct Google account that owns the channel before attempting the connection in Bizntech Social.

# Versión en Español

Si estás intentando conectar un canal de YouTube a Bizntech Social pero no aparece en la lista de conexión, no te preocupes, esta es una situación común. A continuación, te explicamos por qué sucede esto y cómo resolverlo.

### 🔒 **Por qué tu canal puede no estar apareciendo**

Bizntech Social se conecta a YouTube a través de la API oficial de YouTube. Este es el sistema que permite que plataformas externas (como Bizntech Social) se comuniquen de manera segura con YouTube.

Debido a las reglas de la API de YouTube:

- 
Solo el **propietario real del canal** puede conectar un canal de YouTube.

- 
Los usuarios con otros roles (como Manager o Editor en YouTube Studio) no tienen permitido autorizar estas conexiones.

*👉 Esta no es una limitación de Bizntech Social; es una restricción establecida directamente por YouTube para proteger las cuentas y garantizar que solo el propietario principal pueda otorgar acceso.*

### 👩‍💻 **Qué hacer si no eres el propietario del canal**

Si administras un canal de YouTube pero no eres el propietario, no podrás conectarlo a Bizntech Social por tu cuenta. En su lugar, puedes hacer lo siguiente:

- 
Pídele al propietario del canal que inicie sesión en Bizntech Social y conecte la cuenta de YouTube.

- 
Una vez que el propietario conecte el canal, tú (como usuario o Admin en Bizntech Social) podrás igualmente programar, publicar contenido, usar las herramientas de IA para crear publicaciones, revisar métricas y más.

De esta forma, podrás gestionar el canal en Bizntech Social una vez conectado, aunque no hayas sido tú quien autorizó la conexión.

### ✅ **Próximos pasos**

- 
Si no sabes quién es el propietario del canal, revisa en **YouTube Studio > Configuración > Permisos**. Solo la persona listada como **propietario** tiene la capacidad de completar la integración.

- 
Si ya sabes quién es el propietario, simplemente invítalo a tu espacio de trabajo en Bizntech Social como usuario y guíalo para completar el proceso de conexión. [Aprende cómo agregar usuarios a Bizntech Social](#/article/how-to-invite-a-new-user-to-my-company)

✨ **Consejo:** Para evitar problemas de conexión, asegúrate de que el propietario haya iniciado sesión con la cuenta de Google correcta (la que realmente posee el canal) antes de intentar la conexión en Bizntech Social.

## How to Add the Bizntech Social App to Your Phone's Home Screen
**Category**: Accessibility
**Reference ID**: how-to-add-the-bizntech-social-app-to-your-phone-s-home-screen
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
### iOS users. 

Here's a step-by-step guide on how to add the Bizntech Social web app to the home screen of their iPhones or iPads.

**Step 1:**  To begin the process of adding the Bizntech Social app to your home screen, open the Safari browser on your iPhone or iPad.

**Step 2:** In Safari, navigate to the Bizntech Social website. 

**Step 3: ** Once you're on the Bizntech Social website, locate and tap the Action button at the bottom center or top right of the Safari browser window. This button is often represented by a square with an arrow pointing upward.
[](assets/images/media_342.jpeg)
**Step 4:** Select "Add to Home Screen": From the options presented in the action menu, scroll down until you find the "Add to Home Screen" option. Tap on it to proceed.

[](assets/images/media_343.jpeg)

**Step 5**: Give the Web App a Name: A pop-up window will appear, allowing you to give the Bizntech Social web app a name. You can use the default name or enter a custom name of your choice that will appear on your home screen.

**Step 6:** Tap "Add": After entering the desired name, tap the "Add" button in the top right corner of the screen to finalize the process.

### **Android user:**

**Step 1**: Open Chrome on your Android device.

**Step 2: **Navigate to the website or web page associated with the Bizntech Social app.

**Step 3: **Once on the desired page, tap the menu icon located in the upper right-hand corner of the browser window (usually represented by three dots).

**Step 4:** From the menu that appears, select "Add to home screen."

**Step 5:** You'll be prompted to choose a name for the website shortcut. Enter a name that represents Bizntech Social.

**Step 6: **Finally, tap "Add," and Chrome will add the Bizntech Social app shortcut to your Android home screen.

[](assets/images/media_344.jpeg)

​
​

# English Version

## Open AI  *

- 
Login into your [Open AI](https://platform.openai.com/login?launch) account

- 
Go to the API section

- 
At the bottom of the left sidebar, you will see the [API Keys](https://platform.openai.com/api-keys) option. Click in there. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_346.png)
- 
Now click on "+ Create new secret key". Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_347.png)
- 
Put a name to the new key, select a project, and make sure to **allow all permission **for a correct use of the API key. Then click on "Create secret key".

- 
Copy the key and paste it in the [External API Key section](https://bizntechstl.com) inside Bizntech Social. Make sure to paste it in the **OpenAI API Key** option. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_348.png)
- 
Finally, enable the right toggle to start using your API key.

- 
Make sure to **add some credits** to be able to start generating content

Due to OpenAI API restrictions, the latest image models (GPT-Image and GPT-Image-max) are only accessible to registered and approved companies *by OpenAI,* and cannot be used with personal API keys.*

​

## Anthropic  *

Login into your [Anthropic](https://console.anthropic.com/login) account

- 
Select the "[Get API keys](https://console.anthropic.com/settings/keys)" option

- 
Click on "Create Key". Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_350.png)
- 
Put a name on the new key. Then click on "Create Key".

- 
Copy the key and paste it in the [External API Key section](https://bizntechstl.com) inside Bizntech Social. Make sure to paste it in the **Anthropic API Key** option. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_351.png)
- 
Finally, enable the right toggle to start using your API key.

- 
Make sure to **add some credits** to be able to start generating content

## Stability

## Ideogram

##  

## Common API Key Errors

If when creating content you get an alert from Bizntech Social saying that it is impossible to create content, it's essential to troubleshoot common errors related to API key usage. Here are a few key areas to check:

- 
**Toggle Enabled:** Ensure that the feature requiring the API key is toggled on. If it's not enabled, the key won't function as expected.

- 
**Correct API Key:** Double-check that you've entered the correct API key. Even a small typo can prevent the API from functioning.

- 
**Available Credits:** Verify that you still have available credits for API usage. If you've exhausted your credits, the API will stop working until they're replenished.

- 
**Rate Limit:** If your API calls are being throttled, it might be necessary to increase your rate limit. This allows for more requests within a given timeframe.

By reviewing these common errors, you can quickly identify and resolve the issue, ensuring smooth content creation.

## Errores Comunes de Claves API Externas

Si al crear contenido recibes una alerta de Bizntech Social diciendo que es imposible crear contenido, es esencial revisar los errores comunes relacionados con el uso de la clave API externas. Aquí hay algunas áreas clave que debes revisar:

- 
**Interruptor Activado:** Asegúrate de que la función que requiere la clave API esté activada. Si no está habilitada, la clave no funcionará como se espera.

- 
**Clave API Correcta:** Verifica que hayas ingresado la clave API correcta. Incluso un pequeño error tipográfico puede impedir que la API funcione.

- 
**Créditos Disponibles:** Comprueba que aún tengas créditos disponibles para el uso de la API. Si has agotado tus créditos, la API dejará de funcionar hasta que los recargues.

- 
**Límite de Solicitudes:** Si tus solicitudes a la API están siendo limitadas, puede ser necesario aumentar tu límite de solicitudes. Esto permite más solicitudes dentro de un periodo de tiempo determinado.

Revisando estos errores comunes, puedes identificar y resolver el problema rápidamente, garantizando una creación de contenido fluida.

## How to invite a new user to my company
**Category**: Accessibility
**Reference ID**: how-to-invite-a-new-user-to-my-company
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

## Option A: Non whitelable
*
- 
Click on your profile picture at the top right corner.

- 
Click on "[company settings](https://bizntechstl.com)"

- 
Click on the "[access](https://bizntechstl.com)" button. Make sure to be the owner of the company and not an invited user. Only company owners will have access to invite other users. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_400.png)
- 
Click on the "+ Invite user to..." button. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_401.png)
- 
Introduce the email of the user you want to invite to join your company.

If the invited user doesn't have a Bizntech Social account yet, he will receive an email inviting him to create an account.

- 
If the user you just invited already has an account created, he/she will now be able to see the company he/she was invited to in the company menu. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_402.png)

## Option B: With Whitelabel

- 
Go to the [Whitelabel-Management](https://bizntechstl.com) section.

- 
Select the arrow on the right to expand the user menu. Check the image below for reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_403.png)
- 
Click on Add New User to invite someone to join that particular company. Check the image below for reference*👇

- 
A pop-up will appear. Enter the full name, email, and the type of role (admin-user). 

- 
Click on Add User

- 
An email with the invitation link will be sent to that user. 

Each company is managed independently. You can invite a user to one company and not to another. In case you want an invited user to have access to all companies, you must invite him/her individually to each of them.

Invited users have all the same permissions as company owners except for the following sections: 

- 
[Company description](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[External API Keys](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Access settings](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Advanced settings](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Billing section](https://bizntechstl.com) (they can see their own billing section, not the billing section of the company owner)

W*e will be working on implementing more limitations and roles for guest users. Please feel free to [upvote that idea in our feedback section](https://bizntechstl.com.sleekplan.app/feedback/127400).*

# Versión en Español 

## Opción A: Sin Whitelable
*

- 
Haz clic en tu foto de perfil en la esquina superior derecha.

- 
Haz clic en "[Configuración de la empresa](https://bizntechstl.com)".

- 
Haz clic en el botón "[Acceso](https://bizntechstl.com)". Asegúrate de ser el propietario de la empresa y no un usuario invitado. Solo los propietarios de la empresa tendrán acceso para invitar a otros usuarios. Consulta la imagen a continuación para referencia*👇
[*](assets/images/media_404.png)
- 
Haz clic en el botón "+ Invitar usuario a...". Consulta la imagen a continuación para referencia*👇
[*](assets/images/media_405.png)
- 
Introduce el correo electrónico del usuario que deseas invitar a unirse a tu empresa.

Si el usuario invitado aún no tiene una cuenta en Bizntech Social, recibirá un correo electrónico invitándolo a crear una cuenta.

- 
Si el usuario que acabas de invitar ya tiene una cuenta creada, ahora podrá ver la empresa a la que fue invitado en el menú de empresas. Consulta la imagen a continuación para referencia*👇
[*](assets/images/media_406.png)

## Opción B: Con Whitelable

- 
Ve a la sección de [Whitelabel-Management](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
Selecciona la flecha de la derecha para desplegar el menú de usuarios. Mira la imagen a continuación como referencia*👇 
[*](assets/images/media_407.png)
- 
Haz clic en **Agregar nuevo usuario** para invitar a alguien a unirse a esa compañía en particular. Mira la imagen a continuación como referencia*👇 

- 
Aparecerá una ventana emergente. Ingresa el nombre completo, el correo electrónico y el tipo de rol (admin - usuario).

- 
Haz clic en Agregar usuario.

- 
Se enviará un correo electrónico con el enlace de invitación a ese usuario.

Cada empresa se gestiona de forma independiente. Puedes invitar a un usuario a una empresa y no a otra. En caso de que desees que un usuario invitado tenga acceso a todas las empresas, deberás invitarlo/a individualmente a cada una de ellas.

Los usuarios invitados tienen los mismos permisos que los propietarios de la empresa, excepto en las siguientes secciones:

- 
[Descripción de la empresa](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Claves API externas](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Configuración de acceso](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Configuración avanzada](https://bizntechstl.com)

- 
[Sección de facturación](https://bizntechstl.com) (pueden ver su propia sección de facturación, no la sección de facturación del propietario de la empresa)

*Estamos trabajando en la implementación de más limitaciones y roles para los usuarios invitados. [No dudes en votar esa idea en nuestra sección de comentarios.](https://bizntechstl.com.sleekplan.app/feedback/127400)*

## X Premium
**Category**: Accessibility
**Reference ID**: x-premium
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

Bizntech Social supports users on X (formerly Twitter) who have a premium paid account by recognizing their subscription status and enabling access to certain features associated with X Premium. One of the key benefits for premium users is the ability to create posts of up to 25,000 characters.

### Updating Your Subscription Information

If you subscribe to or cancel your X Premium subscription after integrating your account with Bizntech Social, it is important to update the integration. This ensures that X communicates your updated subscription status to Bizntech Social. Without this step, the following may happen:

- 
Changes to your premium status may not be recognized, which could affect your ability to use extended character limits, or on the other hand,

- 
You may be allowed to create posts of up to 25,000 characters but encounter an error when publishing them because you have canceled your X Premium subscription.

### Steps to Update Your Integration

- 
Open the [Integrations](https://bizntechstl.com) section in your Bizntech Social account.

- 
Locate your X (Twitter) account in the list of integrated platforms.

- 
Click on the "*Refresh Integration"* button to refresh the connection.

By following these steps, Bizntech Social will retrieve your current subscription status and apply any associated features automatically.

# Versión en Español

Bizntech Social admite a los usuarios de X (anteriormente Twitter) que tienen una cuenta premium de pago al reconocer su estado de suscripción y habilitar el acceso a ciertas funciones asociadas con X Premium. Uno de los beneficios clave para los usuarios premium es la capacidad de crear publicaciones de hasta 25,000 caracteres.

### **Actualización de tu información de suscripción**

Si te suscribes o cancelas tu suscripción a X Premium después de integrar tu cuenta con Bizntech Social, es importante que actualices la integración. Esto asegura que X comunique tu estado de suscripción actualizado a Bizntech Social. Sin este paso, puede que te pase lo siguiente:

- 
no reconocer los cambios en tu estado premium, lo que podría afectar tu capacidad para usar los límites extendidos de caracteres o por el contrario, 

- 
permitirte crear posteos de hasta 25,000 caracteres pero dar un error a la hora de publicarlos ya que has cancelado tu suscripcion a X premium.

### **Pasos para actualizar tu integración**

- 
Abre la sección de [Integraciones](https://bizntechstl.com) en tu cuenta de Bizntech Social.

- 
Ubica tu cuenta de X (Twitter) en la lista de plataformas integradas.

- 
Haz clic en el botón "*Actualizar integración"* para refrescar la conexión.

Siguiendo estos pasos, Bizntech Social recuperará tu estado de suscripción actual y aplicará automáticamente las funciones asociadas.

## Social Hub
**Category**: Social Hub
**Reference ID**: social-hub
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
Introducing Bizntech Social's Social Hub: Streamline Your Social Media Communication

In today's fast-paced digital landscape, effective communication with your audience is essential for building strong relationships and providing exceptional customer support. Bizntech Social is proud to present the Social Hub feature, a powerful tool designed to revolutionize how you interact with your social media followers. With the Social Hub, you can efficiently manage and respond to messages and comments from your Facebook and Instagram accounts, empowering you to deliver personalized and improved community management.

### Messages

The Messages section of the Social Hub allows you to handle all your incoming messages from Facebook and Instagram in one centralized platform. Say goodbye to switching between multiple apps or platforms to stay on top of your social media conversations. With Bizntech Social's Social Hub, you can conveniently view, respond, and engage with your audience directly from a single location.

By consolidating your message management, you can provide timely and consistent responses, ensuring a seamless customer experience. The Social Hub enables you to handle customer inquiries, address support requests, and nurture leads, all within a user-friendly and efficient interface. Stay organized with a unified inbox and take control of your social media communication like never before.

Additionally, the Social Hub offers the ability to assign messages to specific team members within your company. This feature enhances collaboration and streamlines your customer support processes. Assigning messages allows you to delegate tasks, track progress, and ensure a personalized and efficient resolution for each customer interaction. With Bizntech Social's Social Hub, delivering exceptional support and managing your community becomes a breeze.

### Comments

The Comments section of the Social Hub brings all your post comments from Facebook, Instagram, and LinkedIn into one comprehensive view. You no longer have to visit each social media platform separately to keep track of comments on your posts. Now, you can efficiently manage and respond to comments directly within Bizntech Social's Social Hub.

By centralizing comments, you gain a holistic understanding of engagement on your social media posts. Whether it's a question, a compliment, or feedback, you can engage with your audience promptly and build stronger connections. Stay on top of your comment threads, reply to inquiries, and foster meaningful conversations, all while saving time and effort.

[](assets/images/media_408.png)

It's important to note that, currently, the Social Hub supports message and comment management for Facebook and Instagram only. Due to limitations with the APIs of other social media platforms, this feature is not available for those channels. However, our team is continuously exploring possibilities and working towards expanding the Social Hub's capabilities to include more platforms in the future.

In conclusion, Bizntech Social's Social Hub is a game-changer for managing your social media communication. With its unified inbox for messages and comprehensive comment management, you can effectively engage with your audience, provide top-notch support, and cultivate a thriving online community. Enjoy the convenience, efficiency, and enhanced collaboration offered by Bizntech Social's Social Hub as you take your social media presence to new heights.

## Social Hub Limitations
**Category**: Social Hub
**Reference ID**: social-hub-limitations
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

### Table of contents

Click on a title to have a quick access to the information:

- 
**[Instagram](#h_ea0186259f)**

[Comments](#h_ce4d1a220c)

- 
[Messages](#h_90a5d14c1a)

- 
**[Facebook](#h_bfd49c3f2a)**

[Comments](#h_ee20c6a404)

- 
[Messages](#h_a48f870afc)

You will be able to see new messages and comments on your account after integrating with Facebook and Instagram. Please note that messages and comments created before the integration will not appear in the Social Hub.

⚠️ For long-time users who had a Bizntech Social account before the launch of the Social Hub, it is necessary to refresh the Facebook and Instagram integrations to start seeing new messages and comments in the Social Hub. ⚠️

## Instagram 

Due to restrictions imposed by Meta, the user must always initiate the conversation. It is not possible to start conversations with end-users.

### Comments

- 
It is not possible to reply with Reels.

- 
Only posts that have been published less than 90 days ago will appear 
in this section.​

### Messages

- 
Once a user sends you a message you have 24 hours to respond before the session expires (Instagram API limitation)

- 
You can receive and send emojis as messages inside the conversation

- 
You can receive stickers, but you cannot send them.

- 
You can receive GIFs, but you cannot send them.

- 
Due to Instagram API limitations, it is not possible to delete comments from Bizntech Social.

- 
It is not possible to receive or send multimedia files.

- 
It is not possible to receive or send audio messages.

- 
It is not possible to receive or send emojis inside a selected message.

- 
It is not possible to reply to specific, previously selected messages. 

## Facebook 

Due to limitations imposed by Meta, the user must always initiate the conversation. It is not possible to start conversations with users.

### Comments 

- 
It is not possible to reply with an avatar, multimedia files, GIFs, or stickers.

- 
Comments containing stickers or multimedia files will appear as empty messages in Bizntech Social.

- 
Only posts that have been published less than 90 days ago will appear 
in this section.​
​

### Messages

- 
Once a user sends you a message you have 24 hours to respond before the session expires (Facebook API limitation)

- 
You can receive and send emojis as messages inside the conversation

- 
You can receive static stickers (animated stickers will appear as static), but you cannot send them.

- 
You can receive GIFs, but you cannot send them.

- 
You can receive multimedia files, but you cannot send them.

- 
Due to Facebook API limitations, it is not possible to delete comments.

- 
It is not possible to receive or send audio messages.

- 
It is not possible to receive or send emojis inside a selected message.

Debido a restricciones impuestas por Meta, el usuario debe siempre iniciar la conversación. No es posible iniciar conversaciones con los usuarios finales.

- 
Puedes recibir y enviar emojis como mensajes dentro de la conversación.

- 
Puedes recibir stickers estáticos (los stickers animados aparecerán como estáticos), pero no puedes enviarlos.

- 
Puedes recibir GIFs, pero no puedes enviarlos.

- 
Puedes recibir archivos multimedia, pero no puedes enviarlos.

- 
Debido a limitaciones de la API de Facebook, no es posible eliminar comentarios.

- 
No es posible recibir ni enviar mensajes de audio.

- 
No es posible recibir ni enviar emojis dentro de un mensaje seleccionado.

# CATEGORY: AutoPilot

## Autolisting Rules
**Category**: AutoPilot
**Reference ID**: autolisting-rules
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
## Overview

Welcome to the next step in achieving full automation in social media management with Bizntech Social! We are excited to introduce our new feature: Autolisting Rules. This is the second crucial step towards realizing our vision of Autopilot social media management.

## Recap: The TAGS Feature

Before diving into Autolisting Rules, it's important to revisit our TAGS feature. If you haven't already mastered using tags, we encourage you to do so now, as it plays a critical role in setting up your Autolisting Rules.

[](assets/images/media_411.png)

## How to Create an Autolisting Rule

To automate your posts effectively, you need to have an organized tagging system in place. For this guide, we'll walk you through creating a rule to post every Monday morning on LinkedIn, ensuring your followers are reminded of your presence as the new workweek begins.

## Step-by-Step Guide

- 
**Ensure Your Posts are Tagged:**

Before creating rules, make sure your posts are properly tagged. If you need to review how to tag posts, refer back to our TAGS feature guide.

- 
**Creating the Rule:**

Navigate to the Autolisting Rules section in your dashboard.

- 
Click on "Create Rule."

- 
**Name Your Rule:**

Give your rule a descriptive name, such as "Monday LinkedIn Posts."

- 
**Select Tags:**

Choose the tags you've assigned to the posts you want to automate. For our example, select tags related to professional content for LinkedIn.

- 
**Set Schedule:**

Select the day and time you want the posts to go live. For our scenario, choose Monday morning.

- 
Optionally, you can add a delay to the post.

- 
**Confirm the Rule:**

Click "CONFIRM" to finalize your rule.

[](assets/images/media_412.png)

## Managing Your Autolisting Rule

Once created, you will see your rule in the dashboard marked as "Paused."

- 
**Activate the Rule:**

To activate the rule, click the "Play" button.

- 
**Pause or Edit:**

You can pause the automation at any time by clicking "Pause."

- 
To make changes to your rule, click "Edit."

[](assets/images/media_413.png)

## Conclusion

Autolisting Rules are a powerful tool to streamline your social media posting and ensure consistent engagement with your audience. By effectively utilizing tags and setting up strategic rules, you can maintain a robust social media presence with minimal manual effort.

We hope you find this feature valuable in your journey towards achieving social media automation. As always, if you have any questions or need further assistance, please don't hesitate to reach out to our support team.

Happy automating!
Related Articles

## How to Change Your Email Address
**Category**: Learn How to...
**Reference ID**: how-to-change-your-email-address
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

#### **Step-by-Step Guide to Change Your Email Address**

- 
Click on your profile picture in the top-right corner of the screen.

- 
Select **[User Settings](https://bizntechstl.com)** and then navigate to **Profile**.

- 
Find your current email. Below it, you'll see the option "***Change your login email.*"** Click on it. *Check the image for your reference*👇
[*](assets/images/media_414.png)
- 
Enter your new email address in the designated field.

- 
A confirmation email will be sent to your new email address. Open it and follow the instructions to confirm.

- 
That’s it—you’re all set!

**Make sure you don’t already have a Bizntech Social account associated with the new email address you want to use**. If you do, an error message will appear when you try to make the change. In that case, you’ll need to [delete the account](#/article/how-to-delete-your-account) associated with that email before proceeding with the update.

# Versión en Español

#### **Guía Paso a Paso para Cambiar tu Dirección de Correo Electrónico**

- 
Haz clic en tu foto de perfil en la esquina superior derecha de la pantalla.

- 
Selecciona **[Configuración de usuario](https://bizntechstl.com)** y luego ve a **Perfil**.

- 
Busca tu correo electrónico actual. Debajo, verás la opción **"Cambiar tu correo de inicio de sesión"**. Haz clic en ella. Consulta la imagen para referencia*👇
[](assets/images/media_415.png)
- 
Ingresa tu nueva dirección de correo electrónico en el campo indicado.

- 
Se enviará un correo de confirmación a tu nueva dirección. Ábrelo y sigue las instrucciones para confirmar.

- 
¡Listo, ya está hecho!

**Asegúrate de no tener ya una cuenta de Bizntech Social asociada con el nuevo correo electrónico que deseas usar**. Si es así, aparecerá un mensaje de error cuando intentes realizar el cambio. En ese caso, primero deberás [eliminar la cuenta](#/article/how-to-delete-your-account) asociada a ese correo antes de proceder con la actualización.

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

#### **Step-by-Step Guide to Delete Your Account**

- 
Click on your profile picture in the top-right corner of the screen.

- 
Select **[User Settings](https://bizntechstl.com)** and go to **Advanced**.

- 
Click on **Delete User**. *Check the image below for your reference *👇
[*](assets/images/media_416.png)
- 
A pop-up will appear; follow the instructions to complete the account deletion process.

- 
That’s it—you’re all done!

**⚠️ ATTENTION**
If you are the owner of a subscription plan, it will be immediately canceled, and all users invited to your company will also lose access. You will permanently lose all settings, generated content, assets, scheduled posts, social media integrations, and more. This action cannot be undone.

[](assets/images/media_417.png)

Aparecerá una ventana emergente; sigue las instrucciones para completar el proceso de eliminación de la cuenta.

-

⚠️ **ATENCIÓN**
Si eres el propietario de un plan de suscripción, se cancelará de inmediato y todos los usuarios invitados a tu empresa también perderán acceso. Perderás permanentemente todos los ajustes, contenido generado, archivos, publicaciones programadas, integraciones con redes sociales y más. Esta acción no se puede deshacer.

## How to bulk-schedule posts with CSV Import
**Category**: Learn How to...
**Reference ID**: how-to-bulk-schedule-posts-with-csv-import
**Overview**: 

### Instructions & Details:
# English Version

CSV Import lets you schedule a whole batch of posts across your connected networks at once, straight from a spreadsheet. Instead of creating posts one by one, you build them as rows in a CSV file, upload it, review everything in a single grid, and schedule the entire batch in a few clicks. It lives inside the Calendar.

Use it when you:

- 
Plan your content calendar in a spreadsheet (Google Sheets, Excel, a Notion export).

- 
Need to schedule many posts at once.

- 
Want the same post to go out to several networks at the same time.

### Before you start

- 
**Connect your networks first.** Only networks connected to the company show up in the importer. Add them under **Company Settings → Integrations**.

- 
**Host your media at public URLs.** The importer pulls images and videos from links in your CSV, so each file has to be reachable on the open web.

- 
**Mind the character limits.** Each network has its own caption limit (see the table at the end). The importer flags anything too long before you schedule.

## Step 1: Open the importer

In the left menu, open **Calendar** and click **Import** in the top right, next to *Create Post*.

The CSV Import wizard opens with four steps: **Networks → Upload → Review → Schedule**.

## Step 2: Choose your networks

Pick the networks you want **every row** of your CSV to publish to. Each row becomes one post per selected network.

- 
Click a network to turn it on or off. Use **Select All** / **Unselect All** on the right.

- 
Greyed-out networks are not connected yet. Connect them from Company Settings → Integrations.
[*](assets/images/media_418.jpg)

Tip: the columns and character limits in the next step adapt to the networks you pick here.

## Step 3: Build and upload your CSV

On the **Upload** step:

- 
Click **Download CSV template**. The template already has the right column headers for the networks you selected, plus a couple of example rows.

- 
Fill in one row per post. The only required column is content. Leave anything you do not need blank.

- 
Save the file as **CSV.**

- 
Drag it into the drop area, or click to browse.

The panel on the right is a live reference: required fields, valid values, your actual Pinterest boards, and the character limit for each network you selected.
​
[](assets/images/media_419.jpg)

**Good to know:**

- 
Lines that start with # are treated as comments and ignored.

- 
Leave the date columns (year, month, day) blank to auto-queue a post. Fill them in to schedule for a specific date and time.

#### CSV columns

Column

Required

What it is

Example

content

Yes

The post text / caption. Hashtags and mentions go inline.

Kicking off our launch! #launch

title

No

Optional title, used by networks that have one (Pinterest pin title, YouTube title, Medium).

Summer launch

link

No

Optional link associated with the post (for example, where a Pin points).

[https://yoursite.com](https://yoursite.com)

media_urls

No

One or more public image or video URLs, separated by commas.

[https://site.com/a.jpg,https://site.com/b.jpg](https://site.com/a.jpg,https://site.com/b.jpg)

year

No

Year to publish. Leave blank to auto-queue.

2026

month

No

Month, 1-12.

7

day

No

Day of the month.

10

hour

No

Hour in 24h format, 0-23.

14

minute

No

Minute, 0-59.

0

first_comment

No

Auto-posted first comment, on networks that support it.

Link in bio!

media_product_type

No

The post format: FEED, STORY, REEL or SHORT.

REEL

pinterest_board

No

Which Pinterest board to pin to, by name or ID. Needed for Pinterest.

My Travel Board

tiktok_privacy_level

No

TikTok visibility: PUBLIC, FRIENDS, PRIVATE or FOLLOWERS.

PUBLIC

Some columns only appear if you selected the matching network in Step 2.

**Media tip:** put several URLs in one media_urls cell, separated by commas, to attach more than one image or video (a carousel, on networks that support it). If a URL itself contains a comma, wrap the whole cell in double quotes so your spreadsheet keeps it as one value.

## Step 4: Review and fix

Bizntech Social validates every row against every network you selected. You land on **Review & validate**: a grid where each post shows as one card per network (for example, 2 posts x 3 networks = 6 cards).

- 
A green check means the post is valid for that network. A red X is a blocker you have to fix.

- 
Use the All / Errors / Warnings filters at the top to focus.

- 
Click any row to edit the content, title, schedule or media type inline. Your changes re-validate instantly.

- 
If your text is too long for X (Twitter), click **Auto-trim** to shorten it to the limit automatically.

- 
You cannot continue while there are blockers.

When everything is clear, click **Continue**.

[](assets/images/media_420.jpg)

## Step 5: Schedule

Choose how the batch goes out:

- 
**Add to Queue:** auto-staggers the posts, one per day, at 10am in your timezone. Best when you just want to spread content out without picking dates.

- 
**Use CSV dates:** each post publishes at the exact date and time from its row. Rows with no date fall back to the queue.

- 
**Save as Drafts:** creates unscheduled drafts so you can review and schedule them later.

The Schedule preview shows how many posts land on which days. When you are happy, click **Schedule X posts to Y networks**.

[](assets/images/media_421.jpg)

### After importing

Your posts appear in the **Calendar** and under **My Posts**. Open any one to edit the copy, change the time, or swap the media, exactly like a post you created by hand. Drafts created by the importer stay unscheduled until you give them a time.

### Common issues and limits

- 
**Content too long.** A red X on a network means the caption is over that network's limit. Shorten it inline, or use Auto-trim for X.

- 
**Media not reachable.** Media URLs must be public and load quickly. If a link is private or broken, that row fails. Re-host the file at a public URL.

- 
**A network needs media.** Some networks (for example Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, Pinterest) cannot publish without media. Add a media_urls value, or save those as drafts and attach media later.

- 
**Date in the past.** A row scheduled in the past is blocked. Use a future date, or leave the date columns blank to auto-queue it.

- 
**Columns out of place.** If a cell contains a comma, wrap it in double quotes so the row keeps the right number of columns.

# Versión en español

CSV Import te permite programar un lote entero de posts en todas tus redes conectadas a la vez, directamente desde una planilla. En lugar de crear los posts uno por uno, los armás como filas en un archivo CSV, lo subís, revisás todo en una sola grilla y programás el lote completo en pocos clics. Está dentro del Calendar.

Usalo cuando:

- 
Planificás tu calendario de contenido en una planilla (Google Sheets, Excel, un export de Notion).

- 
Necesitás programar muchos posts de una sola vez.

- 
Querés que el mismo post salga en varias redes al mismo tiempo.

## Antes de empezar

- 
**Conectá tus redes primero.** En el importador solo aparecen las redes conectadas a la company. Agregalas en **Company Settings → Integrations**.

- 
**Alojá tu media en URLs públicas.** El importador toma las imágenes y videos desde los links de tu CSV, así que cada archivo tiene que ser accesible en la web abierta.

- 
**Tené presentes los límites de caracteres.** Cada red tiene su propio límite de texto (ver la tabla al final). El importador marca lo que sea demasiado largo antes de programar.

## Paso 1: Abrir el importador

En el menú de la izquierda, entrá a **Calendar** y hacé clic en **Import**, arriba a la derecha, al lado de Create Post*.

Se abre el wizard de CSV Import con cuatro pasos: **Networks → Upload → Review → Schedule**.

## Paso 2: Elegir las redes

Elegí las redes a las que querés que publique **cada fila** de tu CSV. Cada fila se convierte en un post por cada red seleccionada.

- 
Hacé clic en una red para activarla o desactivarla. Usá **Select All** / **Unselect All** a la derecha.

- 
Las redes en gris todavía no están conectadas. Conectalas desde Company Settings → Integrations.
[*](assets/images/media_422.jpg)

Tip: las columnas y los límites de caracteres del paso siguiente se adaptan a las redes que elegís acá.

## Paso 3: Armar y subir tu CSV

En el paso **Upload**:

- 
Hacé clic en **Download CSV template**. El template ya trae los encabezados correctos para las redes que seleccionaste, más un par de filas de ejemplo.

- 
Completá una fila por post. La única columna obligatoria es content. Dejá en blanco todo lo que no necesites.

- 
Guardá el archivo como **CSV UTF-8**.

- 
Arrastralo al área de carga, o hacé clic para buscarlo.

El panel de la derecha es una referencia viva: campos obligatorios, valores válidos, tus boards reales de Pinterest, y el límite de caracteres de cada red que seleccionaste.
​
[](assets/images/media_423.jpg)

​**Para tener en cuenta:**

- 
Las líneas que empiezan con # se tratan como comentarios y se ignoran.

- 
Dejá las columnas de fecha (year, month, day) en blanco para encolar automáticamente un post. Completalas para programar en una fecha y hora específicas.

#### Columnas del CSV

Columna

Obligatoria

Qué es

Ejemplo

content

Sí

El texto / caption del post. Hashtags y menciones van inline.

Arrancamos el lanzamiento! #launch

title

No

Título opcional, que usan las redes que lo tienen (título del pin de Pinterest, título de YouTube, Medium).

Lanzamiento de verano

No

Link opcional asociado al post (por ejemplo, a dónde lleva un Pin).

[https://tusitio.com](https://tusitio.com)

media_urls

No

Una o más URLs públicas de imagen o video, separadas por coma.

[https://sitio.com/a.jpg,https://sitio.com/b.jpg](https://sitio.com/a.jpg,https://sitio.com/b.jpg)

year

No

Año de publicación. En blanco para encolar automáticamente.

2026

No

7

No

10

No

Hora en formato 24h, 0-23.

No

Link en la bio!

No

El formato del post: FEED, STORY, REEL o SHORT.

REEL

pinterest_board

No

A qué board de Pinterest fijar el pin, por nombre o ID. Necesario para Pinterest.

My Travel Board

tiktok_privacy_level

No

Visibilidad en TikTok: PUBLIC, FRIENDS, PRIVATE o FOLLOWERS.

PUBLIC

Algunas columnas solo aparecen si seleccionaste la red correspondiente en el Paso 2.

**Tip de media:** poné varias URLs en una misma celda media_urls, separadas por coma, para adjuntar más de una imagen o video (un carrusel, en las redes que lo soportan). Si una URL contiene una coma, encerrá toda la celda entre comillas dobles para que tu planilla la mantenga como un solo valor.

## Paso 4: Revisar y corregir

Bizntech Social valida cada fila contra cada red que seleccionaste. Llegás a **Review & validate**: una grilla donde cada post aparece como una tarjeta por red (por ejemplo, 2 posts x 3 redes = 6 tarjetas).

- 
Un tilde verde significa que el post es válido para esa red. Una X roja es un bloqueante que tenés que corregir.

- 
Usá los filtros All / Errors / Warnings de arriba para enfocarte.

- 
Hacé clic en cualquier fila para editar el contenido, el título, el horario o el tipo de media inline. Tus cambios se re-validan al instante.

- 
Si tu texto es demasiado largo para X (Twitter), hacé clic en **Auto-trim** para recortarlo al límite automáticamente.

- 
No podés avanzar mientras haya bloqueantes.

Cuando esté todo en orden, hacé clic en **Continue**.
​
[](assets/images/media_424.jpg)

## Paso 5: Programar

Elegí cómo sale el lote:

- 
**Add to Queue:** escalona los posts automáticamente, uno por día, a las 10am en tu zona horaria. Ideal cuando solo querés distribuir el contenido sin elegir fechas.

- 
**Use CSV dates:** cada post se publica en la fecha y hora exactas de su fila. Las filas sin fecha caen en la cola.

- 
**Save as Drafts:** crea borradores sin programar para que los revises y programes después.

El Schedule preview muestra cuántos posts caen en cada día. Cuando estés conforme, hacé clic en **Schedule X posts to Y networks**.

Captura: Paso 4, elegir cómo se programa el lote.*
​
[](assets/images/media_425.jpg)

### Después de importar

Tus posts aparecen en el **Calendar** y en **My Posts**. Abrí cualquiera para editar el texto, cambiar el horario o reemplazar la media, igual que un post creado a mano. Los borradores que crea el importador quedan sin programar hasta que les asignes un horario.

### Problemas comunes y límites

- 
**Contenido demasiado largo.** Una X roja en una red significa que el caption supera el límite de esa red. Acortalo inline, o usá Auto-trim para X.

- 
**Media inaccesible.** Las URLs de media tienen que ser públicas y cargar rápido. Si un link es privado o está roto, esa fila falla. Volvé a alojar el archivo en una URL pública.

- 
**Una red necesita media.** Algunas redes (por ejemplo Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, Pinterest) no pueden publicar sin media. Agregá un valor en media_urls, o guardá esas filas como borradores y adjuntá la media después.

- 
**Fecha en el pasado.** Una fila programada en el pasado queda bloqueada. Usá una fecha futura, o dejá las columnas de fecha en blanco para encolarla.

- 
**Columnas desfasadas.** Si una celda contiene una coma, encerrala entre comillas dobles para que la fila mantenga la cantidad correcta de columnas.
